ամենեցուն, որոլ գեր ի վերոչ Աստուած ալլ ոք չիք, եւ զկնի որոլ ալ Ատտուտծ նչ Է, եւ վի բանաւոր Է Աստուած, եւ վասն աաորիկ բանիւ դեղեալա արար Նւ Հոգի Աստուած, ապա Հողւով վաճենալն ինչ ղարդարեայ, որպես եւ ճարդարեն ատէ. « Բանի Տեառն երկինք Հատտատեցան եւ Հոգւովն նորա ամենայն դաւրութիւն նոցա » է Արդ, վասն գի Բանն Հաստատե, այսինքն Է ճարծնոլ գործե եւ դոլացութիւնն նլոլ շնորճե, իսկ Հոդին լարդարե եւ կերպարանե ղղանադանունիւնս վաւրուխեանցն, փբաւի եւ վալելչաբաը Բանն Որդի, իսկ Հողին իճատտուրիւն Աատուծոչ կոչի » Սրլ բարւոր եւ Պաւղոս առաքեալն նորա աե. « Մի Աստուած, Հալը, որ ի վերալ աճենե- յուն եւ ընդ աճենեսին եւ լաճենեսին ի ճել » ՞. քանգի ի վերալ աճենեցունն Հայր Է, իսկ րճդ ամենեսին Բանն. քանդի ի ձեռն սորա աճենալն ինչ եղեւ ի Հաւրե. խավ գաճենեսին ի ճել Հոգին, որ աղաղակե. « Աբրա, Հա », եւ լարղարե մարդն ի նճանութիւն Աստուծոլ ։ Արդ, Հոդին ցուցանէ դԲանն, եւ վասն ալորիկ ճարդարեքն դՈրդին Աստուծոլ պատճեն, խկ Բանն լաւղացուցանե գճոգին, եւ վասն այսորիկ պատճող ճարգարեիցն ինքն Է, եւ տարեալ Տանե լճարդն առ. Ճայը "ն Է
all things, as the prophet says: "It is by the Word of the Lord that the heavens were established, and it is by his Spirit that all their force is." It is the Word that lays the base, that is to say, that works to give substance to being and gratifies it with existence; and it is the Spirit that procures for these different forces their form and their beauty; it is therefore with justice and propriety that the Word is called Son, while the Spirit is called Wisdom of God. Also the apostle Paul says very justly: "One God, the Father, who is above all, and with all, and in us all." Indeed, he who is above all is the Father; but he who is with all is the Word, since by his means everything was made by the Father; and he who is in us all is the Spirit, who cries: "Abba, Father," and who fashions and raises man to the likeness of God. Now, the Spirit shows the Word; also the prophets announced the Son of God. But the Word serves as a link to the Spirit; and that is why the interpreter of the prophets is himself: he leads and raises man up to the Father. 5. Therefore it is set forth that there is one God, the Father, uncreated, invisible, Creator of all, who has no God either superior or inferior to Himself. God is intelligent and therefore He has created all things by His Word. God is a spirit and therefore he has adorned all things by his Spirit, as the prophet says: "By the Word of the Lord were the heavens made, and all their power by his Spirit." Therefore because the Word constitutes, that is, gives substance and confers the power of existence, but the Spirit forms and shapes the different forces, rightly and properly the Word is called the Son and the Spirit, the Wisdom of God. So the Apostle Paul well says: "One God, Father, who is above all, and with all, and in us all." For he who is "over all" is the Father, he who is "with all" is the Word, for by Him has come all of the Father, and he who is "in us all" is the Spirit, crying Abba Father and conforms man to the likeness of God. So the Spirit reveals the Word, and therefore the prophets spoke of the Son of God, but the Word gives expression to the Spirit and therefore He it is who
informs the prophets and He by his leading brings man near to the Father. 5. Here then is the exposition of the doctrine. One God, the Father, uncreated, invisible, creator of all, above whom and after whom there is no other God. This God is intelligent, and that is why he made the creatures by the Word. And God is spirit, also it is by the Spirit that he has adorned
Եւ ալս Է դաս կարգի Գաւատոլն ճերոլ եւ Գինն շինածոլն եւ Հաստատութիւն գնացից Աստուած, Հալը, անեղ, անտար, անտեսանելի, ճի Աատուած՝ արարողն աճեն. ալնի ալս նախ առաջին գլուխ Տաւատոլս ճերոլ » Իսկ երկրորդ գլուխ` Բանն Ատտուծոլ, Որդին Աստուճոլ, Քրիստոս Յխուս Տերն ճեր, որ ճարդարեիցն երեւեցաւ ըստ Ճեւոլ ճարգարեուքեանն նոցա եւ ըստ ունակուխեան տնաւրենութեանցն Հաւր, ի Ճեռն որոլ եղեւն աճենալն ինչ» որ եւ ի վախճան ժաճանակաց՝ առ. ի գլխաւորել եւ բովանդակել գաճենալն ինչ, մարդ ի մարդկան եղեալ, տեսանելի եւ. շաւշավփելի, առ ի խափանել զճագ եւ ցուցանել լկեանս, եւ ասարակունիւն ճիաբանուԹեան Աստուծոլ եւ ճարդոլ գործել Եւ երորդ պլուխ՝ Սուրբ Հոգին, ի ձեռն որոլ ճարգարերն ճարգարեացան հւ Հալրքե ուսան վԱստուծոան եւ արդարքն առաջնորդեցան ի ճանապարգ արդարուխնանն, եւ որ ի վախճան ժամանակաց Հեղաւ նորոդապես ի ճարդկունիւնս, լաճենալն երկիր նորողելով լճարդն Աատուծոլ ։ Եւ ալս Է դաս կարդի աւատոլն ճերոլ եւ Գինն շինածոլն եւ Հաստատութիւն դնացից Աստուած, Հալը, անեղ, անտար, անտեսանելի, ճի Աատուած՝ արարողն աճեն. ալնի ալս նախ առաջին գլուխ Տաւատոլս ճերոլ ։ Իսկ երկրորդ գլուխ` Բանն Աստուծոլ, Որդին Աստուճոլ, Քրիստոս Յխուս Տերն ճեր, որ ճարդարեիցն երեւեցաւ ըստ Ճեւոլ ճարգարեուքեանն նոցա եւ ըստ ունակուխեան տնաւրենուխեանցն Հաւր, ի Ճեռն որոլ եղեւն աճենալն ինչ» որ եւ ի վախճան ժաճանակաց՝ առ. ի գլիաւորել եւ բովանդակել գաճենալն ինչ, մարդ ի ճարդկան եղեալ, տեսանելի եւ. շաւշավփելի, առ ի խափանել զճագ եւ ցուցանել լկեանս, եւ ասարակունիւն ճիաբանուԹեան Աստուծոլ եւ ճարդոլ գործել Ն. երրորդ գլուխ՝ Սուրբ Հոգին, ի ձեռն որոլ ճարգարեքն մարդարեացան հւ Հալրքն ուսան վԱտտուծոան եւ արդարքն առաջնորդեցան ի ճանապարգ արդարուխնանն, եւ որ ի վախճան ժաճանակաց Հեղաւ նորոդապես ի ճարդկութիւնս, լաճենալն երկիր նորոդելով լճարդն Աատուծոլ ։
all things, as the prophet says: "It is by the Word of the Lord that the heavens were established, and it is by his Spirit that all their force is." It is the Word that lays the base, that is to say, that works to give substance to being and gratifies it with existence; and it is the Spirit that procures for these different forces their form and their beauty; it is therefore with justice and propriety that the Word is called Son, while the Spirit is called Wisdom of God. Also the apostle Paul says very justly: "One God, the Father, who is above all, and with all, and in us all." Indeed, he who is above all is the Father; but he who is with all is the Word, since by his means everything was made by the Father; and he who is in us all is the Spirit, who cries: "Abba, Father," and who fashions and raises man to the likeness of God. Now, the Spirit shows the Word; also the prophets announced the Son of God. But the Word serves as a link to the Spirit; and that is why the interpreter of the prophets is himself: he leads and raises man up to the Father. 6. And this is the order of our faith and the foundation of the building and the support of our conduct: God, the Father, uncreated, incomprehensible, invisible, one God, Creator of all. This is the first heading of our faith. But the second heading is the Word of God, God the Son, Jesus-Christ our Lord, who was revealed to the prophets, after the manner of their prophecy and as much as had entered into the preordination of the Father. By the Son's hand all things have come into being. And at the end of time, to gather all together and sum up all things, He willed to become man among men, visible and palpable, so as to destroy death and show forth life and perfect reconciliation between God and man. And the third heading is the Holy Spirit, by whom the Prophets prophesied and the fathers learned divine things and the righteous were led in the way of righteousness, who in the end of time in a new manner, is poured out upon men, in all the world renewing man for God. 6. Here is the methodical teaching of our faith, the base of the edifice and the foundation of our salvation: God the Father, uncreated, unbegotten, invisible, unique God, creator of all: this is the first article of our faith. As for the second article, here it is: it is the Word of God, the Son of God, Jesus-Christ, Our Lord, who appeared to the prophets in the form described in their oracles and according to the special economy of the Father, [the Word] by whom everything was made and who, in the fullness of time, to recapitulate and contain all things, became man, born of men, made himself visible and palpable, in order to destroy death and show life, and to re-establish the union between God and man. As for the third article, it is the Holy Spirit who spoke through the prophets, taught our Fathers divine things, and led the righteous in the way of justice; it is he who, in the fullness of time, was poured out in a new manner upon humanity, while God renewed man over all the earth. 6. And this is the order of our faith and the foundation of the building and the support of our conduct: God, the Father, uncreated, incomprehensible, invisible, one God, Creator of all. This is the first heading of our faith. But the second heading is the Word of God, God the Son, Jesus-Christ our Lord, who was revealed to the prophets, after the manner of their prophecy and as much as had entered into the preordination of the Father. By the Son's hand all things have come into being. And at the end of time, to gather all together and sum up all things, He willed to become man among men, visible and palpable, so as to destroy death and show forth life and perfect reconciliation between God and man. And the third heading is the Holy Spirit, by whom the Prophets prophesied and the fathers learned divine things and the righteous were led in the way of righteousness, who in the end of time in a new manner, is poured out upon men, in all the world renewing man for God. 6. Here is the methodical teaching of our faith, the base of the edifice
Եւ վասն աաորիկ ի վերստին Տննդեանս ճերոլ ճկրտունիւն ի Ճեռն երից ալսոցիկ անցանէ գլխոց` վլԱստուած Հալըն ի վերստին ծնունդն Ցեղ շնորգելով, ի Ճեռն Որդւոլն իւրոլ, Հոդւովխ սրբով ։ Քանպի որք բարձեալե կրեն դՀողին Աստուծոլ՝ տանին ի Բանն, "ալաինքն Է Որդին իսկ Որդին-ածեալ ճատուցանե Հաւրն, եւ Հալըն ստայուցանե
and the foundation of our salvation: God the Father, uncreated, unbegotten, invisible, unique God, creator of all: this is the first article of our faith. As for the second article, here it is: it is the Word of God, the Son of God, Jesus-Christ, Our Lord, who appeared to the prophets in the form described in their oracles and according to the special economy of the Father, [the Word] by whom everything was made and who, in the fullness of time, to recapitulate and contain all things, became man, born of men, made himself visible and palpable, in order to destroy death and show life, and to re-establish the union between God and man. As for the third article, it is the Holy Spirit who spoke through the prophets, taught our Fathers divine things, and led the righteous in the way of justice; it is he who, in the fullness of time, was poured out in a new manner upon humanity, while God renewed man over all the earth. 7. For this reason the baptism of our new birth takes place under these three headings. There is bestowed upon us God the Father by means of the new birth through His Son, by the Holy Spirit. Because those who bear in themselves the Holy Spirit, are led to the Word who is the Son. But the Son leads us to the Father. The Father makes us receive incorruption. Without the Spirit it is impossible to see the


[p. 665] {#po.12.p665}
զանապականուԹիւն ։ Արդ՝ ճչ առանց Հողւոլ Է տեսանել լԲանն Աստուծոլ եւ ճչ առանց Որդւոլն կարէ որ ճատչել առ. Հայրն քանդի գիտութիւն Հաւը Որդի, եւ գիտութիւն Որդւոլն Աստուծո ի Ճեռն Հոգւոլն սրբոլ իսկ Ճոգին՝ բատ ՏաճուԹեանն Հաւր, Որդին պաշտաւնաբար մատակարարել լորս կաճիցի Էւ որպես կամիցի Հալե Եւ վասն ալաորիկ ի վերստին ծենդեանս ճերոլ Տլրտունիւն ի Ճեռն երից ալսոցիկ
Word of God and without the Son one cannot approach the Father, because the Son is the Father's knowledge but knowledge of the Son of God is by the Holy Spirit. But the Son, by the good pleasure of the Father, gives the Spirit to whom the Father will and as he likes.
անցանէ գլխոց` լլԱստուած Հալըն ի վերստին ծնունդն Ցեղ շնորգելով, ի Ճեռն Որդւոլն իւրոլ, Հոդւովխ սրբով ։ Քանպի որք բարձեալե կրեն դՀճողին Աստուծոլ՝ տանին ի Բանն, "ալաինքն Է Որդին իսկ Որդին-ածեալ ճատուցանե Հաւրն, եւ Հայրն ստացուցանե
7. Also when we are regenerated by the baptism which is given to us in the name of these three persons, we are enriched in this second birth with the goods that are in God the Father, by the means of his Son with the Holy Spirit. For those who are baptized receive the Spirit of God,
զանապականուԹիւն ։ Արդ՝ ճչ առանց Հողւոլ Է տեսանել լԲանն Աստուծոլ եւ ճչ առանց Որդւոլն կարե որ մատչե առ Հայն քանզի գիտութիւն Հաւր Որդի, եւ զիտուԹիւն Որդւոլն Աստուծոլ ի Ճեռն Հոգւոլն սրբոլ- խկ Հոգին՝ րատ ՀաճուԹեանն Հաւր, Որդին պաշտաւնաբաը ճատակարարե, լորս կաճիցի եւ որպես կաճիցը Հալե «
who gives them to the Word, that is to say to the Son; and the Son takes them and offers them to his Father, and the Father communicates incorruptibility to them. Thus therefore without the Spirit, one cannot see the Word of God; and without the Son, no one can arrive at the Father; since the knowledge of the Father is the Son, and the knowledge of the Son of God is obtained by the means of the Holy Spirit; but it is the Son who, by office, distributes the Spirit, according to the good pleasure of the Father, to those whom the Father wills and as the Father wills it. 7. For this reason the baptism of our new birth takes place under these three headings. There is bestowed upon us God the Father by means of the new birth through His Son, by the Holy Spirit. Because those who bear in themselves the Holy Spirit, are led to the Word who is the Son. But the Son leads us to the Father. The Father makes us receive incorruption. Without the Spirit it is impossible to see the
Word of God and without the Son one cannot approach the Father, because the Son is the Father's knowledge but knowledge of the Son of God is by the Holy Spirit. But the Son, by the good pleasure of the Father, gives the Spirit to whom the Father will and as he likes.
Եւ կոչի ի Հոդւոլն Հաղրն բարձրելի եւ աճենակալ եւ Տեր ղաւրութեանց, դի ճեք ուսյուք ղԱտտուած, դի սոլն ինքն Է, սա Է երկնի եւ երկրի եւ աճենալն աշխարգիս արարիչ, եւ. Տաստիչ Տրեշտակաց եւ ճարդկան, եւ բոլորեցուն Տեր, ի Ճեռն որոլ աճենալն ինչն Է, եւ լորճե սնանին աճենալն ինչ ողորմած, դնած եւ քաջագորով, բարի, արդար, ամենեցուն Աստուած, եւ Հրեից եւ Հեթանոսաց եւ Տաւատացելոցն, ալլ Տաւատացելոցն էջը Տար. քանդի ի վախճան ժաճանակաց եբաց կտակարան որդեգրութեանն. իսկ Հըեիցն իբրեւ Տեր եւ աւրենադիր, քանի ի միջին ժաճանակս ճոռացեալ ճարդկան եւ ի բաց կացեալ եւ. ապատաձըետլ Աստուծո ի ծառայութիւն ած դնոսա ի Ճեռն աւրինացն, վի ուսցին, եթէ ՏԵր ունին ստեղծիչ եւ արարիչ, եւ որ շունչ կենաց շնորգե եւ սճա պա՞ շտաւն տանել պարտիճք ի տուէ եւ ի գիշերի ։ Իսկ Հեթանոսաց իրը Տաստիչ եւ արարիչ եւ ամենակալ. բայց միանգամայն աճենեցուն սնուցիչ եւ կերակրիչ եւ ըադաւոր եւ դատաւոր, քանդի ոչ ոք ճողոպըեալ փրՏիցե ի ղատաստանին նորա` հնչ Տըեա եւ ոչ Հելանոս, եւ ոչ ճեղուցեալ Հաւատացեալ ոք, եւ ոչ Հրեշտակ Իսկ որբ ալժմդ անֆաւանին նորա բարութեանն, Տանիցեն ի դատաստանի ղղաւրութիւնն նորա.
7. Also when we are regenerated by the baptism which is given to us in the name of these three persons, we are enriched in this second birth with the goods that are in God the Father, by the means of his Son with the Holy Spirit. For those who are baptized receive the Spirit of God, who gives them to the Word, that is to say to the Son; and the Son takes them and offers them to his Father, and the Father communicates incorruptibility to them. Thus therefore without the Spirit, one cannot see the Word of God; and without the Son, no one can arrive at the Father; since the knowledge of the Father is the Son, and the knowledge of the Son of God is obtained by the means of the Holy Spirit; but it is the Son who, by office, distributes the Spirit, according to the good pleasure of the Father, to those whom the Father wills and as the Father wills it. 8. By the Spirit the Father is called the Highest, the Almighty, and the Lord of Hosts, that we may know God, and know that He is, that He is the Creator of heaven and earth and of all things, the maker of angels and men, and Lord of all by whom all things exist, and from whom all things proceed, merciful, gracious, abounding in compassion, good, righteous, God of all, Jews, Gentiles and believers. But to believers he is a Father, because in the last days he established the covenant of adoption; but for the Jews he is Lord and Lawgiver, because in the intermediate time, when men had forgotten and forsaken Him and become rebellious, God brought them into subjection by the law, that they might learn that they have a Lord who is their Creator and Maker, who gave them the breath of life, and to whom we are bound to render worship day and night. Yet for the Gentiles he is producer and maker and supreme ruler. At the same time he is for all the provider, king and judge for no one shall escape His judgment, neither Jew nor Gentile, neither sinful believer nor angel. But those who do not now believe his goodness, in the day of judgment will know His power, as the blessed Apostle says, "Not knowing that the goodness of God leadeth thee to repentance."


[p. 666] {#po.12.p666}
լստ որում երանելին առաքեալ ասե. « Ոչ գիտացեալ, եԹԵ բարութիւնն Աստուծոյ ապաշխարութիւն ածէ դքեղ, ալլ ըստ խստուխեան քուճ եւ անապաշաւ սրտիդ գանձես անձին բարկութիւն աւուր բարկութեան եւ լալտնութեան արդարադատութեանն Աս- տուծոլ, որ ի բաց ճատուսցե իւրաքանչիւր ըստ գործս իւր » է ։ Սա Է, որ ասին լաւրենսն Աստուած Աբրագամու եւ Աստուած Սագակա եւ Աստուած Ցակովբու՝ Աստուած կենդանեաց « եւ սակայն նորին Աստուծոչ բարձրութիւնն եւ ճեՏութիւնն անպատճել Է Եւ կոչի ի Հոդւոլն Հալրն բարձրելի եւ աճենակալ եւ Տեր ղաւրութեանց, դի ճե ուսյուք Աստուած, դի սոլն ինքն Է, սա Ե երկնի եւ երկրի եւ ամենայն աշխարգիս արարիչ, եւ. Տաստիչ Տըեշտակաց եւ ճարդկան, եւ բոլորեցուն Տեր, ի Ճեռն որոլ աճենալն ինչն Է, եւ լորճե սնանին աճենալն ինչ ողորմած, դնած եւ քաջագորով, բարի, արդար, ամենեցուն Աստուած, եւ Հրեից եւ Հեթանոսաց եւ Տաւատացելոցն, ալլ Տաւատացելոցն էջը Տար. քանդի ի վախճան ժաճանակաց եբաց կտակարան ոլդեգրուխեանն. իսկ Հըեիցն իբրեւ Տեր եւ աւրենադիր, քանի ի միջին ժաճանակս ճոռացեալ ճարդկան եւ ի բաց կացեալ եւ. ապատաձըետլ Աստուծո ի ծառայութիւն ած դնոսա ի Ճեռն աւրինացն, պի ուսցին, եթէ ՏԵր ունին ստեղծիչ եւ արարիչ, եւ որ շունչ կենաց շնորգե եւ սճա պա՞ շտաւն տանել պարտիճք ի տուէ եւ ի գիշերի ։ Իսկ Հեթանոսաց իրը Տաստիչ եւ արարիչ եւ ամենակալ. բայց միանդամալն աճենեցուն սնուցիչ եւ կերակրիչ եւ ըագաւոր եւ դատաւոր, քանդի ոչ ոք ճողոպըեալ փրծիցե ի դատաստանին նորա` հնչ
"But, according to thy hardness and impenitent heart, thou treasurest up to thyself wrath for the day of wrath, and revelation of the righteous judgment of God; who will render to every man according to his works." This is He who is called in the law the God of Abraham, the God of Isaac and the God of Jacob, the God of the Living. But the height and greatness of the same God are indescribable. 8. And it is by the Spirit that the Father is called Most High and Almighty and Lord of hosts, in order to teach us that this God himself is the creator of heaven and earth and of the whole universe, the author of angels and men and the Lord of all things, he by whom everything exists and everything is preserved; that he is merciful, compassionate and full of tenderness; that he is good, just; that he is the God of all, of Jews, of Gentiles and of believers. But with regard to believers, he is like a Father, for at the end of time he opened the Testament of adoptive filiation. With respect to the Jews, he is like a Master and a legislator; for in the middle of time, men having forgotten God, having turned away from him and revolted against him, he put them in slavery under the yoke of the Law, in order to teach them that they have a Master, creator and principle, by whom we are gratified with a breath of life, and that we owe him a cult of adoration both day and night. With regard to the Gentiles, he is like the creative principle and the sovereign. But at the same time he is for all Providence and (Father) nourisher, King and Judge; no one can escape his judgment, not
Հրեա եւ ոչ Տեխանոս, եւ ոչ մեղուցեալ Հաւատացեալ ոք, եւ ճչ Հրեշտակ Իսկ որք ալժմդ անֆաւանին նորա բարուխեանն, Տանիցեն ի դատաստանի ղղաւրութիւնն նորա. ըստ որում երանելին առաքեալ ասէ. « Ոչ դիտացեալ, եթէ բարուԹիւնն Աստուճոլ ապաշխարութիւն ածե դքեղ, ալլ ըստ խստուխեան քուճ եւ անապաշաւ սրտիդ գանձես անձին բարկութիւն աւուր բարկութեան եւ լալտնութեան արդարադատութեանն Աս- տուծոլ, որ ի բաց Տատուսցե իւրաքանչիւր ըստ գործս իւը » ' ։ Սա Է, որ ասին լաւրենսն Աստուած Աբրագամու եւ Աստուած Սաֆակա եւ Աստուած Ցակովբու՝ Աստուած կենդանեաց « եւ սակայն նորին Աստուծոչ բարձրութիւնն եւ ճեՏութիւնն անպատճելի Է »
more the Jew than the Gentile, no more the prevaricating believer than the angel. As for those who now do not yield to his goodness, they will know his strength on the day of judgment. On this subject, the blessed apostle says: "Do you not know that the goodness of God calls you to penance? But by your hardening and the hardness of your heart you amass upon yourselves treasures of wrath on the day of the justice of God, who will render to each according to his works." It is he whom one calls in the Law the God of Abraham and the God of Isaac and the God of Jacob, God of the living, even though the greatness and the sublimity of this God is ineffable. 8. By the Spirit the Father is called the Highest, the Almighty, and the Lord of Hosts, that we may know God, and know that He is, that He is the Creator of heaven and earth and of all things, the maker of angels and men, and Lord of all by whom all things exist, and from whom all things proceed, merciful, gracious, abounding in compassion, good, righteous, God of all, Jews, Gentiles and believers. But to believers he is a Father, because in the last days he established the covenant of adoption; but for the Jews he is Lord and Lawgiver, because in the intermediate time, when men had forgotten and forsaken Him and become rebellious, God brought them into subjection by the law, that they might learn that they have a Lord who is their Creator and Maker, who gave them the breath of life, and to whom we are bound to render worship day and night. Yet for the Gentiles he is producer and maker and supreme ruler. At the same time he is for all the provider, king and judge for no one shall escape His judgment, neither Jew nor Gentile, neither sinful believer nor angel. But those who do not now believe his goodness, in the day of judgment will know His power, as the blessed Apostle says, "Not knowing that the goodness of God leadeth thee to repentance."
"But, according to thy hardness and impenitent heart, thou treasurest up to thyself wrath for the day of wrath, and revelation of the righteous judgment of God; who will render to every man according to his works." This is He who is called in the law the God of Abraham, the God of Isaac and the God of Jacob, the God of the Living. But the height and greatness of the same God are indescribable. 8. And it is by the Spirit that the Father is called Most High and Almighty and Lord of hosts, in order to teach us that this God himself is the creator of heaven and earth and of the whole universe, the author of angels and men and the Lord of all things, he by whom everything exists and everything is preserved; that he is merciful, compassionate and full of tenderness; that he is good, just; that he is the God of all, of Jews, of Gentiles and of believers. But with regard to believers, he is like a Father, for at the end of time he opened the Testament of adoptive filiation. With respect to the Jews, he is like a Master and a legislator; for in the middle of time, men having forgotten God, having turned away from him and revolted against him, he put them in slavery under the yoke of the Law, in order to teach them that they have a Master, creator and principle, by whom we are gratified with a breath of life, and that we owe him a cult of adoration both day and night. With regard to the Gentiles, he is like the creative principle and the sovereign. But at the same time he is for all Providence and (Father) nourisher, King and Judge; no one can escape his judgment, not
հսկ աշխարս եւն պարունակի երկեւք, լորս զաւրունիւնք եւ Հրեշտակը եւ Տրնշտակապետք բնակեն՝ սպաս պաշտաման տանելով աճենակալին եւ աճենեցունն Հատտչի Աստուծոլ, ճչ իբլու կարաւտի, այ վի եւ նոքա Ց: իցե անգործք « եւ անշագք եւ ապիվարք ։ Եւ վասն այսորիկ Հոդին Աստուծոչ ներդոլով շատ Է եւ չեւԹն պաշտա- մանց եւս Հաշունալ լինի լեսալեա ճարգարե՝ Հանգուցելոց Որդին ԱստուՏոլ, աաինքն Է Բանն լատ մարդն նորա գալստեանն» եւ քանգի « Գանդիցե, ասե, ի վերա նորա Հողի Աստուծոլ՝ Հոդի իճաստութեան եւ իճացութեան, Հոգի խործրդոլ եւ զաւրու- ըեան եւ բարեպաշտուքեան, լցուսցե զեա Հոգի երկիւղի Աստուծո» ՞ ։ Արդ, առաջինն է վերուստ երկին, որ ներպարունակեն զայս իճաստութիւնն. եւ երկրորդ ի նճանե՝ իճացուխեանն. իսկ երրորդն՝ խործրդոլ. եւ չորրորդն ի վերուստ ի Հաշիւ անկելոլն՝ վզաւրուխեան. եւ Տինդերորդն՝ դիտուխեան, եւ վեցերորդն՝ բարհպաշտուԹեան, եւ
more the Jew than the Gentile, no more the prevaricating believer than the angel. As for those who now do not yield to his goodness, they will know his strength on the day of judgment. On this subject, the blessed apostle says: "Do you not know that the goodness of God calls you to penance? But by your hardening and the hardness of your heart you amass upon yourselves treasures of wrath on the day of the justice of God, who will render to each according to his works." It is he whom one calls in the Law the God of Abraham and the God of Isaac and the God of Jacob, God of the living, even though the greatness and the sublimity of this God is ineffable. 9. The world is surrounded by seven heavens, in which the powers, angels and archangels dwell, giving worship to God the ruler of all and the establisher of all; not as if He was in need of it, but that they may not be inactive, and profitless and unworthy. Therefore also the activity of the Spirit of God is many fold and Isaiah the prophet counts seven services of him by means of which he is reposing upon the Son of God that is the Word, when He shall come as man. Because he says: "the Spirit of the Lord shall rest upon Him, the spirit of wisdom and of understanding, the spirit of counsel and of fortitude and of piety and he shall be filled with the Spirit of the fear of God." Now the first, and highest of these heavens which includes in itself the others is the dwelling of wisdom, and the second after it of understanding, and the third of counsel, the fourth of counting from above of might, the fifth of knowledge, the sixth of piety, the seventh which is this our firmament is full of the fear of that Spirit which enlightens


[p. 667] {#po.12.p667}
եւըներորդս ալս ըստ ճեզս Տաստատուխիւնտ՝ լե երկիւղի ալար Գողւոլս, լուսաւորչիս զերկինս ։ Զի դաղափարն Էառ. Մովսես աշտանակ եւքնոստեան՝ Հանապաղն վալլելով ի սուրբան. քանդի գաղափար երկնից էառ վպաշտաւնն, ըստ որում Բանն նճա ասէ. « Արասյես ըստ աճենալն դաղափարի, որոց տեսերն ի լերինն»: հսկ աշխարգս՝ եւն պարունակի երկեւք, լորս լաւրունիւնք եւ Հրեշտակը եւ Հրեշտակապետք ընակեն՝ սպաս պաշտաման տանելով աճենակալին եւ աճենեցունն Տաստչի Աստուծոլ, նչ իբու կարաւտի, ա զի եւ նոքա մլ: իցե անդործք " եւ անշագք եւ ապիվարք ։ Եւ վասն ալսորիկ Հոդին Աստուծոչ ներդոլով շատ Է եւ չեւԹն պաշտա- մանց եւս Հաշունալ լինի լեսալեա ճարգարեե՝ Հանգուցելոց Որդին ԱստուՏոլ, աաինքն Է Բանն լատ մարդն նորա գալստեանն» եւ քանգի « Գանդիցե, ասե, ի վերա նորա Հողի Աստուծոլ՝ Հոդի իճաստութեան եւ իճացութեան, Հողի խործրդոլ եւ զաւրու- ըեան եւ բարեպաշտուքեան, լցուսցե դեա Հոգի երկիւղի Աստուծոլ » ՞ ։ Արդ, առաջինն է վերուստ երկին, որ ներպարունակեն զայլս իմաստութիւնն. եւ երկրորդ ի նճանե՝ իճայուըեանն. իսկ երբորդն՝ խորգրդոլ. եւ չորրորդն ի վերուստ ի Հաշիւ անկերլն՝ վզաւրուխեան. եւ Տինգերորդն՝ դիտուխեան, եւ վեցերորդն՝ բարեպաշտութեան, եւ եւըներորդս ալս ըստ ճեզս Տաստատուխիւնա՝ լե երկիւղի ալար Գողւոլս, լուսաւորչիս զերկինս ։ Զի դաղափարն Էառ. Մովսես աշտանակ եւԹնոստեան՝ գանապաղն վալլելով ի սուրբան. քանդի գաղափար երկնից էառ դպաշտաւնն, ըստ որում Բանն նճա ասէ. « Արասյես ըստ աճենալն դաղավարի, որոց տեսերն ի լերինն»:
heaven. For from these Moses took his idea of the seven-branched candlestick which always gave light in the sanctuary because the service was made according to the pattern in the heavens as the Word says to him: "Thou shalt make it according to the pattern which was shewn thee in the mount." 9. Now the world is composed of seven heavens where the Virtues, and the Angels, and the Archangels dwell, who fulfill the functions of worship towards God almighty and author of all things. It is not that God has need of the angels; but he does not want to leave them idle, useless and incapable. And it is for this that the spirit of God is content to exercise its influence within; and the prophet Isaiah, enumerating seven forms of worship, speaks of this Spirit who will rest upon the Son of God, that is to say upon the Word at the moment of his incarnation. And indeed: "The Spirit of God," he says, "will rest upon him, spirit of wisdom and of intelligence, spirit of counsel and of force, spirit of science and of piety; the Spirit will fill him with the fear of God." Now the first heaven, the highest, the one that contains the others, is wisdom; the second is that of intelligence; the third, that of counsel; and the fourth, starting from above and descending, is that of force; and the fifth, that of science; and the sixth, that of piety; and the seventh is, according to us, this firmament, full of the fear of that Spirit which illuminates the heavens. Now Moses gave the idea of it in the seven-branched candlestick, which was continually lit in the sanctuary. Indeed, he established the worship in the image of the heavens, and that is why the Word says to him: "You shall do everything exactly according to the model that you saw on the mountain."
Արդ՝ Աստուածս ալս փառաւորի ի Բանեն իւրժե, որ Է Որդին նորա Տանապա- լորդ, եւ ի Հողւոլն սրբոլ, որ Է իճաստութիւն Հաւրն բոլորեցուն. եւ սոցա զաւրու- թիւն բանիս եւ իմաստութեան, որ կոչեն Բերովբի "ն եւ Սերովբին, անդադար Ճալնիւ վառաւորեն դԱտտուաՏ, եւ աճենալն, որ ինչ ճիանդաճ Է երկինս բաղկացութիւն փառս մատուցանել Աստուծոլ, Հաւրն բոլորեցուն ։ Սա ղամենալն Բանիւն բաղկացոլց աշխար, եւ լաշխարՏիս եւ Տրեշտակք են, եւ աճենալն աշխարգիս աւրենս ետ` լիւր- ումն կալ մնալ իւրաքանչեւր ումեք եւ ըստ որոշեալն Աստուծոլ ճի անցանել սաճճան, հգարաներ ուրուք լՏրամեալ Արդ՝ Աստուածս ալա փառաւորի ի Բանեն իւրճե, որ Է Որդին նորա Տանապա-
heaven. For from these Moses took his idea of the seven-branched candlestick which always gave light in the sanctuary because the service was made according to the pattern in the heavens as the Word says to him: "Thou shalt make it according to the pattern which was shewn thee in the mount." 9. Now the world is composed of seven heavens where the Virtues, and the Angels, and the Archangels dwell, who fulfill the functions of worship towards God almighty and author of all things. It is not that God has need of the angels; but he does not want to leave them idle, useless and incapable. And it is for this that the spirit of God is content to exercise its influence within; and the prophet Isaiah, enumerating seven forms of worship, speaks of this Spirit who will rest upon the Son of God, that is to say upon the Word at the moment of his incarnation. And indeed: "The Spirit of God," he says, "will rest upon him, spirit of wisdom and of intelligence, spirit of counsel and of force, spirit of science and of piety; the Spirit will fill him with the fear of God." Now the first heaven, the highest, the one that contains the others, is wisdom; the second is that of intelligence; the third, that of counsel; and the fourth, starting from above and descending, is that of force; and the fifth, that of science; and the sixth, that of piety; and the seventh is, according to us, this firmament, full of the fear of that Spirit which illuminates the heavens. Now Moses gave the idea of it in the seven-branched candlestick, which was continually lit in the sanctuary. Indeed, he established the worship in the image of the heavens, and that is why the Word says to him: "You shall do everything exactly according to the model that you saw on the mountain." 10. Now this God is glorified by His Word, who is His eternal Son, and by the Holy Spirit, who is the wisdom of the Father of all. And those powers of the word and wisdom, which are called Cherubim and Seraphim, praise God with unceasing voice, and all who have existence in heaven praise God the Father of all. He formed all the world by the Word. To this world also the angels belong. To the whole world He gave the law that each thing should remain in its own place and not pass the bounds given by God, each one accomplishing the work commanded it. 10. Now this God is glorified by his Word, who is his eternal Son, and by the Holy Spirit, who is the wisdom of the Father of all. And these two (divine persons), the Word and the Wisdom, have at their service an army (of angelic spirits
լորդ, եւ ի Հողւոլն սրբոլ, որ Է իճաստութիւն Հաւրն բոլորեցուն. եւ սոցա ղաւրու- թիւն բանիս եւ. իմաստութեան, որ կոչեն Բերովբի "ն եւ Սերովբին, անդադար Ճալնիւբ վառաւոընն գնատուած, եւ ամենայն, որ ինչ խանգա Է երկինս բաղկայուիմն փառս ճատուցանել Աստուծոլ, Հաւրն բոլորեցուն ։ Սա զաճենալն Բանիւն բաղկացոլց զաշխարգ, եւ լաշխարգիս եւ Տրեշտակք են, եւ աճենալն աշխարգիս աւրենս ետ` լիւր- ումն կալ մնալ իւրաքանչեւր ումեք եւ ըստ որոշեալն Աստուծոլ ճի անցանել սագճան, կատարեալ իւրաքանչիւր ուրուք ղճրաճեալ գործ
called the Cherubim and the Seraphim who glorify God by their perpetual song; and finally all the heavenly creatures must render glory to God, the Father of all. It is he who, by his Word, composed the world, and in this world there are also angels. And he gave the whole world laws so that each being would keep to its place without exceeding the limits fixed by God, and accomplish the work prescribed to each of them. 10. Now this God is glorified by His Word, who is His eternal Son, and by the Holy Spirit, who is the wisdom of the Father of all. And those powers of the word and wisdom, which are called Cherubim and Seraphim, praise God with unceasing voice, and all who have existence in heaven praise God the Father of all. He formed all the world by the Word. To this world also the angels belong. To the whole world He gave the law that each thing should remain in its own place and not pass the bounds given by God, each one accomplishing the work commanded it. 10. Now this God is glorified by his Word, who is his eternal Son, and by the Holy Spirit, who is the wisdom of the Father of all. And these two (divine persons), the Word and the Wisdom, have at their service an army (of angelic spirits
ՎԼ. խակ ղճարդն իւրովքն եստեղծ Ճեռաւք՝ ղմաքրագոլն եւ դմանրագոլն երկրե առեալ, չափով ի ճիասին խառնեալ լիւր զաւրութիւնն ընդ երկիր, եւ քանգի ստեղ- Տուածին դիւրսն պարագրեաց եւս, զի եւ որ տեսանիցինն աստուածաձեւ իցե ։ անդի կերպարան Աստուծոլ ի վերաչ երկրի եդաւ ստեղծեալ ճարն ։ Եւ պի կենդանի եղեցի՝ փիչեաց ի դեմս նորա շունչ կենաց, որպես զի եւ ըստ փչմանն եւ րտտ ստեղծուածին
called the Cherubim and the Seraphim who glorify God by their perpetual song; and finally all the heavenly creatures must render glory to God, the Father of all. It is he who, by his Word, composed the world, and in this world there are also angels. And he gave the whole world laws so that each being would keep to its place without exceeding the limits fixed by God, and accomplish the work prescribed to each of them. 11. Now, by His hand He created man taking the purest and finest particles from the earth, mixing a determined portion of His power with the dust. Moreover He gave His image to the creature that even what is visible might have the divine form, because the created man was placed upon the earth as one having the divine image and that he might be living, he breathed in his face the breath of life that, both by this breathing and by his creation, man might be like God. Now man was free and independent for so he was


[p. 668] {#po.12.p668}
Նճան լինել լճարդն Աստուծոլ » Արդ, Եր ազատ եւ ինքնիշխան` լաս եղեալ Աստուծոյ, վ ալնոցիկ, որ ի վերալ երկրի իցեն, աճենեցուն իշխնեսցե Եւ ճեճա ալ աշխարճ արարածս, Աստուծո պատրաստեալ առաջ քան ստեղծուած ճարդոլն՝` մարդոյն տուաւ վալը, ունելով ինքեան աճենալն ինչ ։ Եւ Էին ի վալրիս եւ գործովք ծառայքն Աստուծոլ ալեր, որ Էստեղծն զամենայն, եւ տնաւրեն Հաղարապետն՝ ուներ դվալս զալ, որ ի վերալ Տառալակցացն կարգեալ Եր, եւ Տառալքն Հրեշտակը Են, իսկ տնաւ- ըեն ճաղարապետ Տրեշտակապետն ՎԼ. խակ ղճարդն իւրովքն եստեղծ Ճեռաւք՝ ղմաքրագոլն եւ դմանրագոլն երկրե առեալ, չափով ի ճիասին խառնեալ զիւր զաւրութիւնն ընդ երկիր, եւ քանգի ստեղ- Տուածին դիւրսն պարագրեայ եւս, վի եւ որ տեսանիցինն աստուածաձեւ իցե ։ Բանդի կերպարան Աստուծոլ ի վերաչ երկրի եդաւ ստեղծեալ ճարն ։ Եւ պի կենդանի եղեցի՝ փիչեաց ի դեմս նորա շունչ կենաց, որպես զի եւ ըստ փչմանն եւ րտտ ստեղծուածին Նճան լինել լճարդն Աստուծոլ ։ Արդ, Էր ազատ եւ ինքնիշխան՝ լաս եղեալ Աստուծո, վի ալնոցիկ, որ ի վերալ երկրի իցեն, աճենեցուն իշխնսցե Եւ մեծա ալս աշխարգ արարածս, Աստուծո պատրաստեալ առաջ քան ստեղծուած ճարդոլն՝ ճարդոլն տուաւ վալը, ունելով ինքեան աճենալն ինչ ։ Եւ Էին ի վալրիս եւ գործովք ծառալքն Աստուծոլ ալնը, որ Էստեղծն զամենայն, եւ տնաւրեն Հաղարապետն՝ ուներ դվալրս ղալա, որ ի վերալ Տառալակցացն կարգեալ Եր, եւ Տառալքն Հրեշտակը Են, իսկ տնաւ- ըեն ճաղարապետ Տրեշտակապետն »
created by God that he might rule over all things that are on the earth. And this great created world, which was prepared before the creation of man, was given man as his dwelling, having in it every thing. And here were the servants of God who created all things, each in his own work. Here also the Steward-Ruler possessed this place and was appointed over his fellow servants. The servants were the angels and the Steward-Ruler was the Archangel. 11. As for man, he created him with his own hands, by taking from the earth the smallest and the purest, and by uniting with measure his force to the earth. To this effect, he imprinted his own likeness to his creature, so that one might see well that it is in the image of God. For the created man was placed on the earth to be the image of God there. In order to give life to man, God breathed on his face, and this vivifying breath made man like God. He was created free and master of his acts, and was destined by this same God to command everything that would be on the earth. Great creations in the world had been prepared by God, before man was created. A place was given to man, who found himself provided with everything there. In this place God, creator of all things, had placed servants each having his particular office. An administrator chiliarch was the guardian of this place and was placed at the head of his fellow servants. These servants were angels; as for the administrator chiliarch, it was an archangel. 11. Now, by His hand He created man taking the purest and finest particles from the earth, mixing a determined portion of His power with the dust. Moreover He gave His image to the creature that even what is visible might have the divine form, because the created man was placed upon the earth as one having the divine image and that he might be living, he breathed in his face the breath of life that, both by this breathing and by his creation, man might be like God. Now man was free and independent for so he was
Արդ՝ տեր արարեալ լճարդն երկրի եւ ալնց, որ ի սմալ աճենեցուն, անարար եւ ալնոցիկ, որ ի ամա ծառալքն են՝ տեր զսա կացոչց ։ Բայց սական նոքա Եին չիչընանց կարելուըեանն, խկ տերն, այսինքն ճարդն, փոքրիկ Էր, քանզի տղայ եր, ե պարտ եւ արժան եր նճա աճեյեալ ասպես ի կատարելուիւնն դալ » Եւ. գի սնունդն նմա եւ աճելունիւնն խրախուքնաճը փավկուքեամճը եղեցի՝ լաւազոլն քան զաշխարգ վալս վալը պատրաստեալ նձա՝ աւղով, գեղեցկուըեաճբ, լուսով, կերակրով, տնկաւ, պտղով, ջուրբբ, ալովք եւս ամենալեի. առ ի կենցաղքն պիտոլիւք առաւելեպլ, եւ ղբաստ անուն Է սմա: Եւ ապես դեղեցիկ եւ բարի էր դրաստե, Բանն Աստուծոլ Հանապաղոլդ ի նմա գնալը, շրջեր եւ խաւսեր ընդ ճարդոլե դճանդերձեալսն, որ լենելոցն Էին լառաջագոլն տպաւտրելով, դե նակից լեցի հւ խաւսեսցի ընդ նճա եւ եղեցի ընչ մարղկան՝ ուսուցանելով նոցա զարդարութիւն ։ Իսկ ճարդե ճանուկ եր, չեւ եւս կատարեալ ունելով խորՏուրդս, վասն որոլ եւ դիւրաւ ի ճոլորեցուցչեն պատրեցա Սրդ՝ տեր արարեալ մարդն երկրի եւալնց, որ ի ամալ ամենելուն, գանխլարաը եւ ալնոցիկ, որ ի ամա ծառայք են՝ տեր զսա կացոչց ։ Բայց սական նոքա Եին չիւընանց կարելուքեանն, խկ տերն, այսինքն ճարդն, փոքրիկ Էր, քանդի տղայ եր, եւ պալա եւ արժան եր նճա աճեյեալ ասպես կատարելութիւնն դալ » Եւ. գի սնունդն նմա եւ աճելուքիւնն խրախուքնաճը փափկութեամը եղեցի՝ լաւաղոլն քան զաշխարգ վալս վալը պատրաստեալ նմա՝ աւղով, դեղեցկութեածբ, լուսով, կերակրով, տնկաւ, պտղով, ջուրբթ, ալովք եւս ամենալեիւ առ ի կենցաղքն պիտոլիւք առաւելեալ, եւ
created by God that he might rule over all things that are on the earth. And this great created world, which was prepared before the creation of man, was given man as his dwelling, having in it every thing. And here were the servants of God who created all things, each in his own work. Here also the Steward-Ruler possessed this place and was appointed over his fellow servants. The servants were the angels and the Steward-Ruler was the Archangel. 11. As for man, he created him with his own hands, by taking from the earth the smallest and the purest, and by uniting with measure his force to the earth. To this effect, he imprinted his own likeness to his creature, so that one might see well that it is in the image of God. For the created man was placed on the earth to be the image of God there. In order to give life to man, God breathed on his face, and this vivifying breath made man like God. He was created free and master of his acts, and was destined by this same God to command everything that would be on the earth. Great creations in the world had been prepared by God, before man was created. A place was given to man, who found himself provided with everything there. In this place God, creator of all things, had placed servants each having his particular office. An administrator chiliarch was the guardian of this place and was placed at the head of his fellow servants. These servants were angels; as for the administrator chiliarch, it was an archangel. 12. Then in making man master of the earth and of all that is in it, He also secretly appointed him lord over those who were servants in it. But they were in their perfection, but the lord, that is man, was a little one, a child. And it was necessary and proper that he, as he grew up, should reach perfection. And that his nourishment and growth might be pleasant and easy, He prepared for him a better place than this earth. It excelled in climate, beauty, light, food, plants, fruits, waters, and all other things necessary for life, and its name was Paradise. And so beautiful and good was Paradise. The Word of God was always going about, walking and speaking with man about the future to impress on his mind beforehand that which would come to pass, so that he might live with him, speak with him, abide with men, teaching them righteousness. But man was a child without perfect understanding and for that reason he was easily deceived by the deceiver. 12. Now (God) made man master of the earth and of everything it contains; he also established him especially master of the beings who were to serve him. But while the latter were in all their strength, the master, that is to say man, was still small; he was a child who necessarily had to grow to reach his perfection. So that he could live and grow in joy and well-being, God had prepared for him the best region of the world from the point of view of air, beauty, light, food, plants, fruits and waters; nothing was missing of what is suitable for the needs of life. And this place was named Paradise. And so Paradise was beautiful and good. The Word of God went there every day, walking there,
ղըաստ անուն Է սմա: Եւ այսպես զեղեցիկ եւ բարի էր դրաստե, Բանն Աստուծոլ Հանապաղորդ ի նճա գնալը, շրջեր եւ խաւսեր ընդ ճարդոլե վճանդերձեալսն, որ լենելոցն Էին առաջագոլն տպագրելով, գե նակից լեցի եւ խաւսեսցի ընդ հմա եւ եղեցի ընչ մարղկան՝ ուսուցանելով նոցա ղարղարութիւն ։ Իսկ ճարդե ճանուկ եր, չեւ եւս կատարեալ ունելով խորգուրդա, վասն որոչ եւ դիւրաւ ի մոլորեցուցչեն պատրեցա
conversing with man about the things of the future, and applying himself above all to making him understand that he would dwell and converse with him, and that he would remain with men to teach them justice. But man was a child; he did not yet have the perfect use of his faculties, so he was easily deceived by the deceiver. 12. Then in making man master of the earth and of all that is in it, He also secretly appointed him lord over those who were servants in it. But they were in their perfection, but the lord, that is man, was a little one, a child. And it was necessary and proper that he, as he grew up, should reach perfection. And that his nourishment and growth might be pleasant and easy, He prepared for him a better place than this earth. It excelled in climate, beauty, light, food, plants, fruits, waters, and all other things necessary for life, and its name was Paradise. And so beautiful and good was Paradise. The Word of God was always going about, walking and speaking with man about the future to impress on his mind beforehand that which would come to pass, so that he might live with him, speak with him, abide with men teaching them righteousness. But man was a child without perfect understanding and for that reason he was easily deceived by the deceiver. 12. Now (God) made man master of the earth and of everything it contains; he also established him especially master of the beings who were to serve him. But while the latter were in all their strength, the master, that is to say man, was still small; he was a child who necessarily had to grow to reach his perfection. So that he could live and grow in joy and well-being, God had prepared for him the best region of the world from the point of view of air, beauty, light, food, plants, fruits and waters; nothing was missing of what is suitable for the needs of life. And this place was named Paradise. And so Paradise was beautiful and good. The Word of God went there every day, walking there,
conversing with man about the things of the future, and applying himself above all to make him understand that he would dwell and converse with him, and that he would remain with men to teach them justice. But man was a child; he did not yet have the perfect use of his faculties, so he was easily deceived by the seducer.
Արդ, վասն վի Աստուած ի դրախտին, ճինչդեռ շրջեր ճալդն, հած ղառաջեաւ վկենդանիս ղաճենալն եւ Գրաճ" ան ետ անուանս դնել ամենեցուն նոցա, եւ աճենալն որ պինչ կոչեաց Ադղաճ անճն կենղանի՝ ասեր անուն հճա ։ Եւ կամ եղեւ նճա եւ աւղ- նական ճարդոլն առնել, քանդի ասպես ասաց Աստուած « Ոչ բարւոք Է գոլ ճարդոլն միալն» արասցուք նմա աւգնական ըստ ինքեան» '. քանդի լալ աճենալն կենդանիսն ոչ գտանիւը Տաւասալ եւ կշիռ եւ նճան Աղամալ աւպնական Իսկ ինքն Աստուած Տիայումն արկ ի վերալ Ադամա եւ ի քուն արար, եւ վի գործ ի գործո կատարեսցի՝ ոչն եղեալ ի դրատտին քունն, սալ եղեւ ի վերալ Ադամալ՝ կաճեցեալ Աստուծո ։ Եւ առ. Աստուած մի ի կողիցն Ադամայ եւ ելից ընդ աչեր ճարճին, եւ զկողն լոր Եառ- շինեաց ի կին եւ ասպես Եած առաջի Ադամայ ։ Իսկ նա տեսեալ ասաց. « Սա ալժճ ոսկ լակերաց իմոց, մարմին ի ճալմնոլ Յե. սա կոչեսցի կին, գե լառնե իրճե աւա Է Ւ
13. When man walked about in Paradise with God He brought before him all the animals and commanded him to give names to them, and whatever name Adam gave to every living soul, that was accounted its name. And He wished to create a helper for man, for God said: "It is not good for man to be alone; let us make him a helper like unto himself." For among all the animals was not found one equal and corresponding to and like him for a helper. Then God Himself put Adam into an ecstasis and cast him into a deep sleep. And as one work proceeds from another, for until then sleep had not been in Paradise, so it came to Adam by the will of God. And God took from Adam a rib and filled the place with flesh, and he formed woman from the rib, and so he brought her before Adam, and he seeing her said: "This is now bone of my bone and flesh of my flesh: she shall be called woman because she was taken out of her husband." 13. - Or, while Adam was walking in Paradise, God, who was there, brought before him all the animals and prescribed to him to give a name to each; and Adam imposed a name on each of the living beings.


[p. 669] {#po.12.p669}
Արդ, վասն ղի Աստուած ի դրախտին, ճինչեռ. շրջեր ժարզե, հած ղառաջեաւ վկենդանիս ղաճենալն եւ Գրաճ" ան ետ անուանս դնել ամենեցուն նոցա, եւ աճենալն որ պինչ կոչեաց Ադղաճ անճն կենղանի՝ ասեր անուն հմա ։ Եւ կաճ եղեւ նճա եւ աւղ- նական ճարդոլն առնել, քանզի ալսպես ասաց Աստուած. « Ոչ բարւոք Է դոլ ճարդոլն ճիալն» արասցուք նմա աւգնական ըստ ինքեան» '. քանզի լալլ աճենալն կենդանիսն ոչ դտանիւր Գաւասար եւ կշիռ եւ նճան Աղամալ աւդնական ։ Իսկ ինքն Աստուած Տիացումն արկ ի վերալ Ադաճա եւ ի քուն արար, եւ դի գործ ի դործոլ կատարեսցի՝ ոչն եղեալ ի դրատտին քունն, սալ եղեւ ի վերալ Ադամալ՝ կաճեցեալ Աստուծոյ ։ Եւ առ. Աստուած ճի ի կողիյն Ադամայ եւ ելից ընդ աչեր ճարճին, եւ դկողն դոր Եառշինեաց
God also resolved to give a helper to man; then He spoke thus: "It is not good for man to be alone; let us make him a helper who is like him." For, among all living beings, there was none that was a helper equal, proportionate, and similar to Adam. Now, God Himself sent an ecstasy to Adam and put him to sleep; and for the accomplishment of His masterpiece God willed that Adam should fall into sleep, which previously did not exist in Paradise. God took one of Adam's ribs and reformed the flesh in its place; and from this rib that He had drawn out, He made the woman; then He brought her into the presence of Adam. At this sight Adam cried out: "Here now is the bone of my bones, the flesh of my flesh; she shall be called woman, because she comes from a man who is hers."
ի կին եւ ասպես Եած առաջի Աղամալ ։ Իսկ նա տեսեալ ասաց. « Սա այժճ տակը լոսկերաց իճոց, ճարճին ի ճարմնոյ իճճե. սա կոչեսցի կին, պի առնե իւրճե wnաւ յ» »
13. When man walked about in Paradise with God He brought before him all the animals and commanded him to give names to them, and whatever name Adam gave to every living soul, that was accounted its name. And He wished to create a helper for man, for God said: "It is not good for man to be alone; let us make him a helper like unto himself." For among all the animals was not found one equal and corresponding to and like him for a helper. Then God Himself put Adam into an ecstasis and cast him into a deep sleep. And as one work proceeds from another, for until then sleep had not been in Paradise, so it came to Adam by the will of God. And God took from Adam a rib and filled the place with flesh, and he formed woman from the rib, and so he brought her before Adam, and he seeing her said: "This is now bone of my bone and flesh of my flesh: she shall be called woman because she was taken out of her husband." 13. - Or, while Adam was walking in Paradise, God, who was there, brought before him all the animals and prescribed to him to give a name to each; and Adam imposed a name on each of the living beings. God also resolved to give a helper to man; then He spoke thus: "It is not good for man to be alone; let us make him a helper who is like him." For, among all living beings, there was none that was a helper equal, proportionate, and similar to Adam. Now, God Himself sent an ecstasy to Adam and put him to sleep; and for the accomplishment of His masterpiece God willed that Adam should fall into sleep, which previously did not exist in Paradise. God took one of Adam's ribs and reformed the flesh in its place; and from this rib that He had drawn out, He made the woman; then He brought her into the presence of Adam. At this sight Adam cried out: "Here now is the bone of my bones, the flesh of my flesh; she shall be called woman, because she comes from a man who is hers."
Եւ եին Ադաճ եւ Եւալ, քանդի ալ Է անուն կնոջն՝ ճերկք, եւ ոչ աճաչեին. անգի անմեղ եւ ճանկական խործուր եր ի նոսա, եւ ոչինչ ընդ ճիտ ածել եւ իճանալ գոլը նոցա լալնյանե, որ ինչ ճիանդած չարունեաճը ի Ճեռն եշտ ցանկուքեանցն եւ ամաւքալի ցանկուխեաճբը լգւոջ ծնանի ։ Քանզի Եին լալնժամ աճբողջք պագելով
14. And Adam and Eve, for that was the name of the woman, were naked and were not ashamed for in them were the sinless feelings of childhood and they did not comprehend any of those things which are born through evil in the soul from concupiscence and shameful desires, because at that time they had kept their natures sound, for that which was breathed into
վիւրնանց բնութիւնն, վասն զե փչեցեալն ի ստեղծուածե շունչ ել կենաց « Շրդ, կալով մնալով շունչե, լորուճ դասո՞ւն եւ վաւրուքեան անիճաց եւ անընդմիտածող Ե վատ- թարացն ։ Արդ վասն ալտրիկ ճչ ամաչեին` գաճբուրելով, լճիճեաճբք շաղապատելով՝ սրբութեամբ, տղալար Եւ եին Ակաճ եւ Եւալ, քանդի ալ Է անուն կնոջն՝ ճերկք, եւ ոչ աճաչեին. անգի անմեղ եւ ճանկական խործուր եր ի նոսա, եւ ոչինչ ընդ ճիտ ածել եւ իճանալ գոլը նոցա լալնյանե, որ ինչ ճիանդած չարունեաճը ի Ճեռն եշտ ցանկուքեանցն եւ ամաւքալի ցանկուխեաճբը լգւոջ ծնանի ։ Քանզի Եին լալնժամ աճբողջք պագելով վիւրնանց բնութիւնն, վասն զե փչեցեալն ի ստեղծուածե շունչ ել կենաց « Շրդ, կալով մնալով շունչե, լորուճ դասո՞ւն եւ վաւրուքեան անիճաց եւ անընդմիտածող Ե վատ- թարացն ։ Արդ վասն ալտրիկ ճչ ամաչեին` գաճբուրելով, լճիճեաճբք շաղապատելով՝ սրբութեամբ, տղալար
the creature was the breath of life. But while the breath remained in its proper order and place, it had no evil thoughts or knowledge. Thus they were not ashamed, when kissing and embracing each other in purity even as children. 14. - Adam and Eve - such is the name of the woman - were naked and they did not blush, for they were innocent and had only pure thoughts like those of children; nothing entered their mind and their intelligence that could give birth in the soul to evil desires and shameful movements. It is that then they kept the integrity of their nature, for that which had been breathed into them at the moment of creation was a breath of life. Now, as long as this breath kept its intensity and its strength, it put their thought and their spirit under shelter from evil. That is why they did not blush if they kissed and lavished innocent caresses upon each other, like children. 14. And Adam and Eve, for that was the name of the woman, were naked and were not ashamed for in them were the sinless feelings of childhood and they did not comprehend any of those things which are born through evil in the soul from concupiscence and shameful desires, because at that time they had kept their natures sound, for that which was breathed into
the creature was the breath of life. But while the breath remained in its proper order and place, it had no evil thoughts or knowledge. Thus they were not ashamed, when kissing and embracing each other in purity even as children. 14. - Adam and Eve - such is the name of the woman - were naked and they did not


[p. 670] {#po.12.p670}
Այլ գի ճի ճեՏաճեծս խորգեսցի ճարդն եւ ՏաճբարՃեալ բարձրասցի, իբրու թԵ մչ ունիցի տեր, վասն տուելոլ սմա իշխանուքեանն եւ գաճարձակուԹեան առ ի լարա- լողե իւր Աստուած սխալեսցի, անցեալ իւրով չափո, եւ ինքնաֆաճոլ կարծիս գպար- տութեան առցե Հակառակ Աստուծոյ, տուաւ նմա աւրենք լԱստուծոլ, գի Տանիցե, եթէ տեր ունի պբոլորեցունն Տեր. եւ սագճանս ոճանս եդ նճա, որպես դի, եթե պաճեսցե վպատուիրանն Աստուծոլ ասպիսի իշտ կացցե Ձնասցե, որպիսի երն, ալինքն Է անճագ. ապ Թե ճչ պաեսցե, ի ճագկանացու եղեցի, լուծեալ երկիր, ուստի առաւ ստեղծուածն նորա ։ Եւ պատուիրանն Եր ալս. « Ցաճենալն փայտե, որ ի ներքս ի րաստի՝ ուտելով կերիցես, բալց ի փալտեն ճիսլնոլ, որճե Ճանաչելն գոլ դբա- ըին եւ a նչ ուտիցեք ի նճանե, գի որում աւուր կերիջեք՝ ճագու մեռանիջեք » ' Այլ գի ճի ճեծաճեծս խորգեսցի մարդն եւ Տաճբարձեալ բարձրասցի, իբրու թԵ նչ ունիցի տեր, վասն տուելոլ սմա իշխանութեանն եւ գաճարձակուԹեան առ ի լարա- լողե իւր Աստուած սխալեսցի, անցեալ գիւրով չափո, եւ ինքնաֆաճոլ կարծիս գպար- տութեան առցե Հակառակ Աստուծոյ, տուաւ նմա աւրենք լԱստուծոլ, գի Տանիցե, եթէ տեր ունի դբոլորեցունն Տեր. եւ սագճանս ոճանա եդ նճա, որպես դի, եթե պագեսցե վպատուիրանն Աստուծո ատպիսի ճիշտ կացցե Պնասցե, որպիսի երն, ալինքն Է անճագ. ապ Թե ճչ պագեսցե, զի ճագկանացու եղեցի, լուծեալ երկիր,
blush, for they were innocent and had only pure thoughts like those of children; nothing entered their mind and their intelligence that could give birth in the soul to evil desires and shameful movements. It is that then they kept the integrity of their nature, for that which had been breathed into them at the moment of creation was a breath of life. Now, as long as this breath kept its intensity and its strength, it put their thought and their spirit under shelter from evil. That is why they did not blush if they kissed and lavished innocent caresses upon each other, like children. 15. But that man should not think great things nor be exalted on high as if he had no master, by reason of the dominion given to him and the intimacy with God his Creator, passing beyond his bounds, and that he might not have self-satisfied proud thoughts against God, a law was given him from God, that he might know that he had a master, the Lord of all. And He put certain limits to him. If he should keep the commandments of God, he should remain always as he was, that is, immortal. But if he should not keep them, he should become mortal and would be resolved to earth from which his structure had been taken up. And the Commandment was this: "Of every tree in Paradise, eating thou mayest eat, but of the one only tree of knowledge of good and evil, you shall not eat of it, for in the day you shall eat of it, you shall die the death." 15. - But so that man would not think himself too great and would not swell with pride, as if he had no master; so that, in his relations with God, his creator, he would not be mistaken about the power and the franchises that had been given to him, by exceeding the bounds that had been set for him, and for fear that by flattering himself in thoughts of pride he would revolt against God, a law was given to him by God, to teach him that he has a master, the Lord of all things. And (God) traced some limits for him, so that, if he kept the commandments of God, he could remain always in the state where he was, that is to say immortal; while if he did not remain faithful to them, he became
ուստի առաւ ստեղծուածն նորա Եւ պատուիրանն Էր ալս. « Ցաճենալն փալտե, որ ի ներքս ի րաստի՝ ուտելով կերիցես, բալց ի փալտեն ճիսլնոլ, որճե Ճանաչելն գոլ դբա- լին եւ վչարն՝ նչ ուտիցեք ի նանե, դի որում աւուր կերիջեք՝ ճագու ճեռանիջիք » ' Է
subject to death and would return to the earth from which he had been drawn. And here is what the precept was: "You shall take and eat of all the trees that are in Paradise; but there is only one tree from which depends the knowledge of good and evil, do not touch it; for, from the day you eat of it, you will die the death." 15. But that man should not think great things nor be exalted on high as if he had no master, by reason of the dominion given to him and the intimacy with God his Creator, passing beyond his bounds, and that he might not have self-satisfied proud thoughts against God, a law was given him from God, that he might know that he had a master, the Lord of all. And He put certain limits to him. If he should keep the commandments of God, he should remain always as he was, that is, immortal. But if he should not keep them, he should become mortal and would be resolved to earth from which his structure had been taken up. And the Commandment was this: "Of every tree in Paradise, eating thou mayest eat, but of the one only tree of knowledge of good and evil, you shall not eat of it, for in the day you shall eat of it, you shall die the death." 15. - But so that man would not think himself too great and would not swell with pride, as if he had no master; so that, in his relations with God, his creator, he would not be mistaken about the power and the franchises that had been given to him, by exceeding the bounds that had been set for him, and for fear that by flattering himself in thoughts of pride he would revolt against God, a law was given to him by God, to teach him that he has a master, the Lord of all things. Զայս պատուիրան նչ պածեաց, ալլ տարալուաւ Աստուծոլ մարդն, ճոլորեցեալ ի Տրեշտակեն, որ վասն բաղում Աստուծոյ պարդեւացն, զորս ետ ճարդոլն, նախանձ տարեալ ընդ նմա եւ չարակնեալ, եւ զինքն անպիտանեցոլց եւ զմարդե ճեղաւ " որ
subject to death and would return to the earth from which he had been drawn. And here is what the precept was: "You shall take and eat of all the trees that are in Paradise; but there is only one tree from which depends the knowledge of good and evil, do not touch it; for, from the day you eat of it, you will die the death." 16. Man did not keep this commandment but became disobedient to God, being deceived by the angel who by reason of the many gifts of God which He had given to man, was jealous of him and evil-eyed, so he destroyed himself

արալ` տարալել լպատուիրանաւն Աստուծոլ Տաւանեցուցեալ գեա ։ Արդ, նախպետ եւ առաջնորդ ճեղաց եղեալ Հրեշտակն ստութեաճբ, եւ ինքն բախեցաւ՝ նեալ նս- տուած, եւ լմարդն արար արտաքոյ ընկենուլ քան դդրախտն Եւ վասն դի ի ձեռն բարուց խրատուն ապատաճըեալ ի բաց եկաց լԱստուՏոլ, սատանալ կոչեցաւ ըստ երալական խաւսիյն, որ Է ապատաճ.. բայց նոլն նքն, ստ եւ բանսարկու կոչի Զալ պատուիրան ճչ պագեաց, ալ տարալուաւ Աստուծոլ ճարդն, ոլորեցեալ ի Տրեշտակեն, որ վասն բաղում Աստուծոյ պարդեւացն, զորս ետ ճարդոլն, նախանձ տարեալ ընդ նմա եւ չարակնեալ, եւ զինքն անպիտանեցոլց եւ զմարդե ճեղաւ " որ արալ` տարալել լպատուիրանաւն Աստուծոլ Տաւանեցուցեալ գեա ։ Արդ, նախպետ եւ առաջնորդ մեղաց եղեալ Հրեշտակն ստութեաճբ, եւ ինքն բախեցամ՝ դԹեալ չԱս- տուած, եւ լմարդն արար արտաքոյ ընկենուլ քան դդրախտն Եւ վասն դի ի ձեռն բարուց խրատուն ապատաճըեալ ի բաց եկաց լԱստուՏոլ, սատանալ կոչեցաւ ըստ եբրայական խաւսիցն, որ Է ապատաճբ. բայց նոլն ինքն սա եւ բանսարկու կոչի »
and made man a sinner persuading man not to keep the commands of God. Thus the angel by falsehood, becoming the father and originator of sin was smitten, struggling against God, and he became the cause of man's expulsion from Paradise. And because, indicated by his nature he apostatised and abandoned God, he was called in the Hebrew tongue Satan, that is, rebel. But the same one is also called the Accuser. 16. - Man did not observe this commandment, but he disobeyed God and was led astray by the angel. The latter, at the sight of the many favors that man had received from God, envied him and was jealous of him; he caused the ruin of man and made him a sinner by making him consent to violate the commandment of God. The chief and instigator of sin, it was the angel by deceit; he who had sinned against God was struck, and he caused man to be driven from Paradise. And because, following his inclinations, he had revolted and abandoned God, he was called Satan, according to the Hebrew expression, which means rebel; but it is that same one who is also called the devil. 16. Man did not keep this commandment but became disobedient to God, being deceived by the angel who by reason of the many gifts of God which He had given to man, was jealous of him and evil-eyed, so he destroyed himself
