Արդ՝ եթե ոչ ծնաւ, եւ ոչ մեռաւ. եւ եթէ ոչ ճեռաւ, եւ ոչ լարեաւ ի մեռելոց. եւ եթէ ոչ լարեաւ ի մեռելոց, ոչ Է լաղնեալ մագու, եւ ոչ խավանեալ ըազաւորութիւն նորա. եւ էր ճա ոչ աղթեալ, ղեմլդ Ցեք ի վեր ելցութ ի կեանտ՝ ի սկղբանեքս ընդ
manifested Himself to us in the very places where we lost life and broke the bonds in which we were engaged. His light appeared to us, it dissipated the darkness of our prison and it purified the sources of our life, abolishing death and breaking the chains in which we were entwined. And by operating His own resurrection, He became Himself the firstborn from the dead, He resurrected in Himself fallen man and made him ascend to the highest of the heavens, to the right hand of the glory of the Father. Thus God had promised it by His prophet in these terms: "And I will raise up the lost tent of David," that is to say the body that He held from David. That is what Our Lord Jesus Christ truly accomplished, by operating our salvation gloriously: it is through Him that His Father saves us and resurrects us truly. And if someone does not admit that He was born of a Virgin, how will he admit His resurrection from the dead? For there is nothing astonishing, nor wonderful, nor strange in the fact that He who did not have this birth, was not resurrected from the dead; it would even be impossible for us to speak of resurrection with regard to Him; since the Unborn, the Immortal, the Being exempt from (temporal) birth cannot fall under the blows of death. For He who would not have had a beginning as man, how could He have the same end? 39. If He was not born then He did not die, and if He did not die then He did not rise from the dead, and if He did not rise from the dead then He has not conquered death nor destroyed its kingdom. And if He has not conquered death, how can He raise unto life those who have fallen from the beginning


[p. 689] {#po.12.p689}
մաճուաճբ անկեալք ։ Արդ՝ որ արտաքս Հանեն զփրկութիւն ի ճարդոլն եւ ոչ Հաւատան Աստուծո, եԹԵ լարուսցե զնոսա ի ճեռելոց, նոքա եւ լծնունդն Տեառն ճերոլ անգոսնեն, վոր վասն մերն կրեաց, Բանն Աստուծոլ ճարճին լինել, զի ճարճնոլն չարութիւն ցուցցե, եւ ամենեսեան լառաջասցի երկինս. զի նախածին անդրանիկ խործրդոլն Հաւր: Բան կատարեալ` ղամենալն ինչ նաւաստելով եւ աւրենադրելով ինքն ի վերալ երկրի. վզի անդրանիկ Էր կուսին, արդար, մարդ սուրբ, աստուածապաշտ, բարի, Գաճոլ Աստուծով, կատարեալ աճենալնիւ, ապրեցուցանելով զամենեսին, որք զետ երթան նորա ի դժոխոց. դի անդրանիկ ճեռելոց ինքն եւ նախապետ առաջնորդ կենացն » Արդ՝ էԹԷ ոչ Տնաւ, եւ ոչ ճեռաւ. եւ եթէ ոչ ճեռաւ, եւոչ լարեաւ ի ճեռելոց եւ եթէ ոչ արեաւ ի մեռելոց, ոչ Է լաղեալ ճագու, եւ ոչ խափանեալ թագաւորութիւն Նորա. եւ եթէ մագ ոչ լաղնեալ, ղիմրդ մեք ի վեր ելցուբ ի կեանս՝ ի սկղբանեքս րնդ մաճուաճբ անկեալք ։ Արդ՝ որ արտաքս Հանեն զփրկութիւն ի ճարդոլն եւ ոչ Հաւատան Աստուծո, եԹԵ լարուսցե զնոսա ի ճեռելոց, նոքա եւ լծնունդն Տեառն մերոլ անգոսնեն, զոր վասն մերն կրեաց, Բանն Աստուծոլ ճարճին լինել, զի ճարճնոլն չարութիւն ցուցցե, եւ ամենեսեան լառաջասցի երկինս. ղի նախածին անդրանիկ խործրդոլն Հաւր: Բան կատարեալ` ղամենալն ինչ նաւաստելով եւ աւրենադրելով ինքն ի վերալ երկրի. ղի անդրանիկ Էր կուսին, արդար, մարդ սուրբ, աստուածապաշտ, բարի, Գաճոլ Աստուծոյ, կատարեալ աճենալնիւ, ապրեցուցանելով զամենեսին, որբ վգետ երթան նորա ի դժոխոց. դի անդրանիկ ճեռելոց ինքն եւ նախապետ առաջնորդ կենացն »
under death. But they who put salvation far from man and do not believe that God will give them resurrection from the dead they also despise the birth of our Lord which He endured for us. The Word of God became flesh that He might show the resurrection of the body and go to heaven before all; for the Word, the first begotten, the first born of the counsel of the Father, fulfilled all things, sailed through all, and gave a law on the earth. For He was the first born of the Virgin, a righteous man, holy, godly, good, pleasing to God, perfect in every thing, Saviour from hell of all who follow him, for He is the first born from the dead, and the principle leader to the life of God. 39. If therefore He is not born, He is not dead either; and if He is not dead, He is not resurrected from the dead either; and if He is not resurrected from the dead, He has not triumphed over death and has not destroyed its empire; and if He has not triumphed over death, how shall we be able to rise to life, we who, from the beginnings, have fallen under the blows of death? Now, those who do not admit the salvation of man, who do not believe that God must resurrect them from the dead, those despise also the birth of Our Lord. The Word of God, having deigned to become flesh, accepted this birth for us, in order to prove the resurrection of the flesh and to precede us all to heaven. For He is the very first-born in the counsel of the Father, the perfect Word, governing everything and regulating everything by Himself on the earth; He is the first-born of the Virgin, a righteous man, holy, worshiper of God, good, pleasing to God, perfect in everything, saving from hell all those who walk in His footsteps: He is the first-born from the dead and the principal guide who leads to the life of God. 39. If He was not born then He did not die, and if He did not die then He did not rise from the dead, and if He did not rise from the dead then He has not conquered death nor destroyed its kingdom. And if He has not conquered death, how can He raise unto life those who have fallen from the beginning
under death. But they who put salvation far from man and do not believe that God will give them resurrection from the dead they also despise the birth of our Lord which He endured for us. The Word of God became flesh that He might show the resurrection of the body and go to heaven before all; for the Word, the first begotten, the first born of the counsel of the Father, fulfilled all things, sailed through all, and gave a law on the earth. For He was the first born of the Virgin, a righteous man, holy, godly, good, pleasing to God, perfect in every thing, Saviour from hell of all who follow him, for He is the first born from the dead, and the principle leader to the life of God. 39. If therefore He is not born, He is not dead either; and if He is not
« Արդ՝ ալսպես առաջանալ լամենեսին Բանն Աստուծոլ, զի ճարդ ճշճարիտ Է եւ սքանչելի խործրդական եւ Աստուած Գզաւր՝ ի գասարակութիւն ճիաբանութեան դճարդն անդրեն վերստին կոչեցեալ Աստուծոչ, պի ի Ճեռն առ նալն Հասարակուխեան ճեր վկցորդութիւն անապականութեանն աոցուք » Արդ որ քարոզեալն լինի աւրինացն ի ձեռն Մովսեսի եւ մարդարեիցն Աստուծոլ բարձրելոլ եւ աճենակալի, Որդւոլ Հաւրն բոլորեցուն, լործէ ամենալն ինչ. որ խաւսեցաւն ընդ Մովսեսի՝ սա եկն ի Հրեաստան, սերճանեալ Աստուծոլ ի ձեռն Հոգւոլն սրբոչ. եւ ծնեալ ի կուսեն Մարիաճալ լալն- ճանե, որ ի զաւակեն Դաւխի եւ Աբրագաճու՝ Յիսուս աւծեալն Աստուծո, ցուցանելով
dead, He is not resurrected from the dead either; and if He is not resurrected from the dead, He has not triumphed over death and has not destroyed its empire; and if He has not triumphed over death, how shall we be able to rise to life, we who, from the beginnings, have fallen under the blows of death? Now, those who do not admit the salvation of man, who do not believe that God must resurrect them from the dead, those despise also the birth of Our Lord. The Word of God, having deigned to become flesh, accepted this birth for us, in order to prove the resurrection of the flesh and to precede us all to heaven. For He is the very first-born in the counsel of the Father, the perfect Word, governing everything and regulating everything by Himself on the earth; He is the first-born of the Virgin, a righteous man, holy, worshiper of God, good, pleasing to God, perfect in everything, saving from hell all those who walk in His footsteps; He is the first-born from the dead and the principal guide who leads to the life of God. 40. So the Word of God precedes all, for He is truly man, the wonderful counsellor, the mighty God, who again renewedly invites man to the community of harmony with God that by means of this union, we may receive the impartation of incorruption? So He who was preached by Moses in the Law and by the Prophets of the Most High and Almighty God, the Son of the Father of all, from whom are all things, who spoke with Moses, He came to Judea, being the seed of God by means of the Holy Spirit and being born of that Virgin Mary who was of the seed of Abraham and David, Jesus the anointed of God who showed Himself as the one who was preached beforehand by the prophets.
ղենքն գոլ դլառւաջ քարողեալն ի՞ ձեռն մարզարելցե. « Արդ՝ ալսպես առաջանալ լամենեսին Բանն Աստուծոլ, զի ճարդ ճշճարիտ Է եւ սքանչելի խործրդական եւ Աստուած Գզաւը՝ ի գասարակութիւն ճիաբանութեան դճարդն անդրեն վերստին կոչեցեալ Ատտուծոչ, պի ի Ճեռն առ նալն Հասարակութեան մեր վկցորդութիւն անապականութեանն աոցուք » Արդ որ քարոլեալն լինի աւրինացն ի ձեռն Մովսեսի եւ ճարդարեիցն Աստուծոլ բարձրելոլ եւ աճենակալի, Որդւոլ Հաւրն բոլորեցուն, լորճէ ամենալն ինչ. որ խաւսեցաւն ընդ Մովսեսի՝ սա եկն ի Հրեաստան, սերճանեալ Աստուծոլ ի Ճեռն Հոգւոյն սրբոչ. եւ ծնեալ ի կուսեն Մարիաճալ լալն- ճանեչ որ ի զաւակեն Դաւխի եւ Աբրագամու` Յիսուս աւծեալն Աստուծոյ, ցուցանելով ղինքն գոլ դլառւաջ քարողեալն ի՛ ձեռն ճարդարեիցն.
40. And it is thus that the Word of God has precedence over everything; for being true man, at the same time as the admirable counselor and the mighty God, He has again called man to enjoy intimate union with God, so that, thanks to this communion with Him, we may participate in His incorruptibility. Now, He who is announced in the Law by Moses and by the prophets of the Most High and almighty God, the Son of the Father of all by whom everything was made, He who spoke with Moses, that one came to Judea, He who was divinely conceived by the operation of the Holy Spirit and was born of the Virgin Mary, issued herself from the race of David and Abraham, it is the Anointed of God, Jesus who proved that He is indeed (the Messiah) announced in advance by the prophets. 40. So the Word of God precedes all, for He is truly man, the wonderful counsellor, the mighty God, who again renewedly invites man to the community of harmony with God that by means of this union, we may receive the impartation of incorruption? So He who was preached by Moses in the Law and by the Prophets of the Most High and Almighty God, the Son of the Father of all, from whom are all things, who spoke with Moses, He came to Judea, being the seed of God by means of the Holy Spirit and being born of that Virgin Mary who was of the seed of Abraham and David, Jesus the anointed of God who showed Himself as the one who was preached beforehand by the prophets.
40. And it is thus that the Word of God has precedence over everything; for being true man, at the same time as the admirable counselor and the mighty God, He has again called man to enjoy intimate union with God, so that, thanks to this communion with Him, we may participate in His incorruptibility. Now, He who is announced in the Law by Moses and by the prophets of the Most High and almighty God, the Son of the Father of all by whom everything was made, He who spoke with Moses, that one came to Judea, He who was divinely conceived by the operation of the Holy Spirit and was born of the Virgin Mary, issued herself from the race of David and Abraham, it is the Anointed of God, Jesus who proved that He is indeed (the Messiah) announced in advance by the prophets.
ԳԼ. որոլ կարապետ նախընթաց Ցովճաննես Մկրտիչ լառաջադոլն լարդարելով եւ կազճելով ժողովուրդն նկալութիւն Բանին կենաց, ղսա զեկուցեալ գոլ լԲրիտոոսն, լոր վերալ Աստուծոլ Հոդին Հանգեաւ, խառնեալ ընդ ճարմնոլ նորա ։ Սորա աշակերտք եւ վկայք ամենալն բարի գործոցն, եւ վարդապետուխեանն եւ չարչարանացն նորա, եւ ճագուն եւ լարուխենանն եւ դլերկինասն ետ ճարճնաւոր լարութեանն ելե` առաքեալքն, որք լետ ղաւրուխեան Սուրբ Հողւոն առաքեցեալք ի նճանե լաճենալն աշխարճ, "զճեխանոսայ կոչումն արարին՝ ցուցանելով ճարդկան ղճանապարծն կենաց, դարձու- չանել զնոսա ի կռոց եւ ի պոռնկութենե եւ լաւելաստացութենե, ճաքրելով զանձինա նոցա եւ լճարճինս ի Ճեռն ճկրտութեանն ջըոլ եւ Հոգւոլն Սրբոլ, զոր Հոգի Սուրբ. առնն ի Տեառնէ, դա բաժանեալ եւ բաշխեալ Հաւատացելոցն, եւ ալտպես կարգեալ աստա- տեցին եկեղեցիս Հաւատով եւ սիրով եւ լուսով Հատտատեցին առաջ ըարողեցեալն ի ճարգարեից՝ ղկոչումն Հեթանոսացն, ըստ ողորմութեան Աստուծո եղեալ ի վերալ նոցա, ի ձեռն իւընանց արբանեկուքեան սպասուն երեւեցուցանելով, եւ առ րնդունելով էլ բնանց արլ: լ եմեցուցանելով, Նւ. առընդունել նոսա ի խոստումն Գարցն՝ ալսպեսն Տաւատացելոց եւ սիրողացն վՏեր, եւ ի սրբու- թեան եւ գարդարունեան եւ ի ամբերուխնան վայրին կեանս լաւիտենականտ, ի Ճեռն լարուխեանն ի ճեռելոց, մատուցանել լնատուածն բոլորեցուն խոստացեալ՝ ի Ճեռն
41. His Forerunner John the Baptist, prepared beforehand and made ready the people to receive the Word of life, and made Him known as the Christ upon whom the Spirit of God descended touching His body. His disciples, the witnesses of all his good works, teachings, suffering, death, and resurrection and bodily ascension to heaven after His bodily resurrection, are the Apostles who with the power of the Holy Spirit, were sent into the whole world by Him to call the Gentiles, showing to men the way of life, turning them back from idols and fornication and avarice cleansing their souls and bodies by the baptism of water and the Holy Spirit, which Holy Spirit they received from the Lord to impart to and confer upon believers and so doing they established the Church. With faith, hope and love they established the calling of the Gentiles, which was before preached by the prophets according to the mercy of God which was upon them, by the help of the service which they showed. And they received those who believed in the Lord and loved Him, into the promise of the fathers and into holiness, righteousness and patience which lead to eternal life by means of resurrection from the dead. Because God has promised to grant resurrection to all by the one who made and rose again by Jesus Christ, to whom he trusted the Kingdom over all things, the dominion over living and dead and the judgment.
ալեր, որ ճեռաւն եւ լարեաւն՝ Յիսուսի Բրիստոսի, լորուճ ընձեռեաց դեիցս բոլորեցուն լխագաւորուխիւն եւ զիշխանութիւն՝ կենդանեացն եւ ճեռելոց. եւ դատաստանն, եւ վզմարճինն պաճել ի լարութիւնն անշաղախ եւ անձն անապական` բանիւ Ճշճարտու- ըեան խրատ ետուն ։ Լ. որոլ կարապետ նախրնթաց Ցովճաննես Մկրտիչ լառաջադոլն լարդարելով եւ կազմելով ղժողովուրդն րնկալունիւն Բանին կենաց, զսա զեկուցեալ գոլ գ րիստոսն, լոր վերալ Աստուծոլ Հոդին Հանգեաւ, խառնեալ ընդ ճարմնոլ նորա ։ Սորա աշակերտք եւ վկայք ամենալն բարի գործոցն, եւ վարդապետուխեանն եւ չարչարանացն նորա, եւ ճագուն եւ լարուխենանն եւ դլերկինսն ետ ճարմնաւոր լարութեանն ելե` առաքեալքն, որք լետ լաւրուխեան Սուրբ Հոդւոն առաքեցեալք ի նմանէ լաճենալն աշխար, "զճեխանոսայ կոչումն արարին՝ ցուցանելով ճարդկան ղճանապարգն կենաց, դարձու- չանել զնոսա ի կռոց եւ ի պոռնկութենե եւ լաւելաստացութենե, ճաքրելով զանձինա Նոցա եւ լճարճինս ի Ճեռն ճկրտուԹեանն ջըոլ եւ Հոգւոլն Սրբոյ, զոր Հոգի Սուրբ. առնն ի Տեառնէ, ա բաժանեալ եւ բաշխեալ Հաւատացելոցն, եւ ալտպես կարգեալ աստա- տեցին եկեղեցիս Հաւատով եւ սիրով եւ լուսով Հատտատեցին դլառաջ ըարողեցեալն ի ճարգարեից՝ զկոչումն Հեթանոսացն, ըստ ողորճութեանն Աստուծո եղեալ ի վերալ նոցա, ի Ճեռն իւրեանց արբանեկուցեան սպասուն երեւեյուցանելով, եւ առ ընդունելով նոսա ի խոստումն Գարցն՝ ալսպեսն Տաւատացելոց եւ սիրողացն լՏեր, եւ ի սրբու- թեան եւ գարդարուխեան եւ ի ամբերուխեան վայրին կեանս լաւիտենականտ, ի Ճեռն լարուխեանն ի ճեռելոց, մատուցանել լնատուածն բոլորեցուն խոստացեալ՝ ի Ճեռն ալեր, որ ճեռաւն եւ արեաւն՝ Յիսուսի Բրիստոսի, լորուճ ընձեռեաց դեիցս բոլորեցուն լխագաւորունիւն եւ դիշխանութիւն՝ լենդանեացն եւ ճեռելոց. եւ լդատաստանն, եւ վզմարճինն պաճել ի լարութիւնն անշաղախ եւ անձն անապական` բանիւ Ճշճարտու- ըեան խրատ ետուն ։
And they exhorted by the word of truth that the body be kept blameless and the soul uncorrupted for the resurrection. 41. He (Our Lord Jesus Christ) had as forerunner and as precursor John the Baptist, whose mission was to prepare and to form the people for the reception of the Word of life. And John the Baptist declared that Christ was that very one upon whom rested in a sensible manner the Spirit of God. Disciples of Jesus and witnesses of all His good works and of His teaching, witnesses of His passion, of His death, of His resurrection and of His ascension to heaven after His resurrection according to the flesh, the apostles, fortified by the Holy Spirit, were sent by Him into the whole world to operate the vocation of the Gentiles, to show men the path of life, to tear them away from idolatry, from fornication and from greed, and to purify their souls and their bodies by the baptism of water and of the Holy Spirit. The apostles therefore, after having distributed and communicated to the believers this Holy Spirit that they had themselves received from the Lord, regulated things thus and established the Church. By spreading faith, charity and hope, they realized what had been announced in advance by the prophets, the vocation of the Gentiles; thus they have, by the help of their ministry, made this effect of the mercy of God burst forth, which was to admit the Gentiles to participate in the promises made to the Fathers. They attested that to those who believe these truths, who love the Lord and who live in holiness, justice and patience, the God of all will grant eternal life by the resurrection of the dead, and that in view of the merits of Him who died and rose again, Jesus Christ, to whom He gave universal royalty and the power to judge the living and the dead. The apostles, by preaching the word of truth, instructed (the faithful) to keep their body pure for the resurrection and to preserve their soul without stain. [II. - Demonstration of the truth of the apostolic teachings.] 43. But in all things it is just and necessary to believe in the word of God, for God is truthful in everything. One must believe particularly that there is a Son of God and that He exists not only at the moment when He is going to appear to the world, but even before the creation of the world. Moses, who first predicted Him, expresses himself thus in Hebrew: "Baresit bara Elovim basan benouam samenthares," which, in the Armenian language, means: "The Son (was) in the beginning; God created then the heaven and the earth." This is also what the prophet Jeremiah certifies in these terms: "I have begotten thee before the morning star and thy name is before the sun," and it is before the creation of the world, since the stars were formed at the same time as the world. And the same (prophet) says again: "Happy is he who was before becoming


[p. 690] {#po.12.p690}


[p. 691] {#po.12.p691}
man." For God, in effect, the Son was in the beginning before the creation of the world, but for us, it is since He appeared to us. Beforehand for us, He was not, for we did not know Him. And it is for that reason that His disciple John, declaring to us that He is the Son of God, that He was with the Father before the world was, and that it is through Him that all creatures exist, speaks thus: "In the beginning was the Word and the Word was with God, and the Word was God. He was in the beginning with God; everything was made by Him and without Him nothing was made." He demonstrates evidently that He who, in the beginning, was the Word with the Father, He through whom everything was made, is indeed the same who is His Son.
41. His Forerunner John the Baptist prepared beforehand and made ready the people to receive the Word of life, and made Him known as the Christ upon whom the Spirit of God descended touching His body. His disciples, the witnesses of all His good works, teachings, suffering, death, and resurrection and bodily ascension to heaven after His bodily resurrection, are the Apostles who, with the power of the Holy Spirit, were sent into the whole world by Him to call the Gentiles, showing to men the way of life, turning them back from idols and fornication and avarice, cleansing their souls and bodies by the baptism of water and the Holy Spirit, which Holy Spirit they received from the Lord to impart to and confer upon believers, and so doing they established the Church. With faith, hope, and love they established the calling of the Gentiles, which was before preached by the prophets according to the mercy of God which was upon them, by the help of the service which they showed. And they received those who believed in the Lord and loved Him, into the promise of the fathers and into holiness, righteousness, and patience which lead to eternal life by means of resurrection from the dead. Because God has promised to grant resurrection to all by the one who made and rose again by Jesus Christ, to whom He trusted the Kingdom over all things, the dominion over living and dead and the judgment.
And they exhorted by the word of truth that the body be kept blameless and the soul uncorrupted for the resurrection. 41. He (Our Lord Jesus Christ) had as forerunner and precursor John the Baptist, whose mission was to prepare and form the people for the reception of the Word of life. And John the Baptist declared that Christ was the very one upon whom the Spirit of God rested in a sensible manner. Disciples of Jesus and witnesses of all his good works and his teaching, witnesses of his passion, his death, his resurrection, and his ascension to heaven after his resurrection according to the flesh, the apostles, strengthened by the Holy Spirit, were sent by him into the whole world to effect the calling of the Gentiles, to show men the way of life, to tear them away from idolatry, fornication, and greed, and to purify their souls and bodies by the baptism of water and the Holy Spirit. The apostles, therefore, after having distributed and communicated to the believers this Holy Spirit which they had themselves received from the Lord, thus regulated matters and established the Church. By spreading faith, charity, and hope, they realized what had been announced in advance by the prophets, the calling of the Gentiles; thus they, by the help of their ministry, made manifest this effect of the mercy of God, which was to admit the Gentiles to participate in the promises made to the Fathers; they attested that to those who believe these truths, who love the Lord and who live in holiness, justice, and patience, the God of all will grant eternal life through the resurrection of the dead, and this in view of the merits of him who died and rose again, Jesus Christ, to whom he gave universal royalty and the power to judge the living and the dead. The apostles, in preaching the word of truth, instructed (the faithful) to keep their body pure for the resurrection and to preserve their soul without stain. [II. - Demonstration of the truth of the apostolic teachings.] 43. But in all things it is just and necessary to believe the word of God, for God is truthful in everything. One must believe particularly that there is a Son of God and that he exists not only at the moment when he is about to appear to the world, but even before the creation of the world. Moses, who first predicted him, expresses himself thus in Hebrew: "Baresit bara Elovim basan benouam samentharès", which, in the Armenian language, means: "The Son (was) in the beginning; God then created heaven and earth." This is also what the prophet Jeremiah certifies in these terms: "I have begotten thee before the morning star and thy name is before the sun and it is before the creation of the world, since the stars were formed at the same time as the world." And the same (prophet) says again: "Blessed is he who was before becoming."
Քանի ալսպես ունել լգաւատացեալսն` կացեալ" մնացեալ լնը ի նոսա Հոգին Սուրբ, որ տուեալ լենի ի ճկրտութեանն ի նճանե եւ պաճի լառողեն՝ Ճշճարտութեաճբ եւ սրբուխեաճը եւ արդարուեաճը եւ Հաճբերութեաճը վարեցեալ. քանգի աաը ոզւոլ լարունիւն լինի Հաւատացելոցն՝ ճարմնոլ անդրեն ընդունելով դանՃնն եւ Հանդերձ ընդ նճա ղաւրուխեաճը Հոդւոլն Սրբոլ, լարուցելոլ եւ ի ներքս ճուծելոլ լարքալութիւնն Աստուճոլ ։ Ալս Է աւրգնութեանն ՑափեԹի պտուղն ի կոչճանն Հեանոսաց, ի ձեռն եկեղեցւոլ երեւեալ, կացեալ անսացեալ ղբնակութիւնն ընդունել ի տուն Սեճա ըստ խոստճանն Աստուծոյ ։ Զաճենալն լալսոսիկ աապես եղոցիլ լառաջաղոլն ղեկոլդ Հոդին Աստուծոլ ի Ճեռն ճարդարեիցն, առ ի Հաստատուն առ նոսա լՏաւատսն լինել ճշմարտուխեաճբ. Աստուածն պաշտողայ. քանդի որ ինչ ճիանգաճ անկարութիւն Էլ ճերոլ բնութեանս, եւ վասն ալորիկ վատագաւատուԹիւն Հանդերձեալ Եր ճատուցանել ճարդկան` զալաոսիկ Աստուած ի Ճեռն ճարգարեիցն արար լառաջագոլն ղեկուցանիլ՝ վի ի լառաջադունեն ասիր յառաջ բաղում ժամանակաւ, ապա Հուսկ լետոլ ղելսն
man. For God, indeed, the Son was in the beginning before the creation of the world, but for us, it is since he appeared to us. Before that for us, he was not, for we did not know him. And it is for this that his disciple John, declaring to us that he is the Son of God, that he was with the Father before the world was, and that it is by him that all creatures exist, speaks thus: "In the beginning was the Word and the Word was with God, and the Word was God. He was in the beginning with God; all things were made by him and without him nothing was made." He demonstrates evidently that he who, in the beginning, was the Word with the Father, he by whom all things were made, is indeed the same who is his Son. 42. For so it is necessary that believers keep themselves, the Holy Spirit always dwelling in them as He is given at the time of the baptism by Him and is kept by the recipient who lives in truth, holiness, righteousness, and patience. For it is by this Spirit that the resurrection comes to believers, the body again receiving the soul and united with it by the power of the Holy Spirit is raised to enter the kingdom of God. This is the fruit of the blessing of Japheth, in the calling of the Gentiles, displayed by means of the Church which was expecting to receive a habitation in the house of Shem, according to the promise of God. That all this must be so, the Spirit of God had made known by the Prophets, so that faith might be established by the truth in them, that is, in the true worshippers of God. For that which was impossible to our natures and therefore would cause unbelief in man, God made known beforehand by the prophets, that by foretelling it, that is, a long time beforehand, and by its being fulfilled at the end as God had foretold, we might know that it was God that beforehand revealed to us our salvation. 42. - But so that believers may keep themselves thus, it is necessary that the Holy Spirit
առնուլ ալսպես, որպես եւ լառաջագոլն ասացեալ եղեւ՝` Տանիցուր՝ զի Աստուած Էր, որ լառաջադոլնն պատճեայ ճեղ ղփրկութիւնն ճեր ։ Քանզի այսպես ունել լՏաւատայեալսն՝ կացեալ ` մնացեալ լն ի նոսա Հողին Սուրբ, որ տուեալ լինի ի ճկրտութեանն ի նճանե եւ պածի լառողեն՝ ճշճարտութեամը եւ սրբուխեաճը եւ արդարուխեաճը եւ Հաճբերութեաճը վարեցեալ. քանգի աաը ոզւոլ լարունիւն լինի Հաւատացելոցն՝ ճարմնոլ անդրեն ընդունելով դանՃնն եւ Հանդերձ ընդ նճա ղաւրուխեաճը Հոդւոլն Սրբոլ, լարուցելոլ եւ ի ներքս ճուծելոլ լարքալութիւնն Աստուճոլ ։ Ալս Է աւրգնութեանն Ցափեի պտուղն ի կոչճանն Հեանոսաց, ի ձեռն եկեղեցւոլ երեւեալ, կացեալ անսացեալ ղբնակութիւնն ընդունել ի տուն Սեճա ըստ խոստճանն Աստուծոյ ։ Զաճենալն լալսոսիկ աապես եղոցիլ լառաջաղոլն ղեկոլդ Հոդին Աստուծո ի Ճեռն մարգարեից, առ ի Հաստատուն ատ. նոսա վծաւատան լինել ճշմարտուխեաճբ. Աստուածն պաշտողայ. քանդի որ ինչ ճիանգաճ անկարութիւն Էլ ճերոլ բնուԹեանս, եւ վասն ալորիկ վատագաւատութիւն Հանդերձեալ Եր ճատուցանել մարդկան` զայսոսիկ Աստուած ի Ճեռն ճարգարեիցն արար լառաջագոլն ղեկուցանիլ՝ վի ի լառաջադունեն ասիր յառաջ բաղում ժամանակաւ, ապա Հուսկ լետոլ դելսն առնուլ ասպես, որպես եւ լառաջագոլն ասացեալ եղեւ՝` Տանիցուր՝ զի Աստուած Էր, որ լառաջադոլնն պատճեայ ճել ղփրկութիւնն եր ։
remains closely united to them. Given by God at baptism, the Holy Spirit remains in him who receives it as long as he lives in truth and in holiness, in justice and in patience. For it is by the virtue of this Spirit that believers will rise again, when the body will be again united to the soul and will enter into the kingdom of God. Such are therefore the fruits produced by the blessing of Japheth; it is the calling of the Gentiles, manifested by means of the Church, and which makes them enter and dwell in the house of Shem, according to the promise of God. By the voice of the prophets, the Holy Spirit has announced in advance that all this would be so, in order to strengthen the faith in those who worship God in truth. For that which is absolutely impossible to our nature and for this reason could provoke doubt among men, God had it announced in advance by the prophets. From the moment that the thing is announced long in advance and that it is finally accomplished such as it was predicted, we can conclude that it is God alone who revealed it to us in advance for our salvation. 42. For so it is necessary that believers keep themselves, the Holy Spirit always dwelling in them as He is given at the time of the baptism by Him and is kept by the recipient who lives in truth, holiness, righteousness, and patience. For it is by this Spirit that the resurrection comes to believers, the body again receiving the soul and united with it by the power of the Holy Spirit is raised to enter the kingdom of God. This is the fruit of the blessing of Japheth, in the calling of the Gentiles, displayed by means of the Church which was expecting to receive a habitation in the house of Shem, according to the promise of God. That all this must be so, the Spirit of God had made known by the Prophets, so that faith might be established by the truth in them, that is, in the true worshippers of God. For that which was impossible to our natures and therefore would cause unbelief in man, God made known beforehand by the prophets, that by foretelling it, that is, a long time beforehand, and by its being fulfilled at the end as God had foretold, we might know that it was God that beforehand revealed to us our salvation. 42. - But so that believers may keep themselves thus, it is necessary that the Holy Spirit
Իսկ Աստուծոլ աճենալնի՞ Տաւատալ պարտ եւ արժան Է. քանդի ճշծարիտ Է լամենալնի Աստուած, եւ ղի եղեւ Որդի Աստուծով, եւ սա Է ոչ ճիալն գառաջ բան
remains closely united to them. Given by God at baptism, the Holy Spirit remains in him who receives it as long as he lives in truth and in holiness, in justice and in patience. For it is by the virtue of this Spirit that believers will rise again, when the body will be again united to the soul and will enter into the kingdom of God. Such are therefore the fruits produced by the blessing of Japheth; it is the calling of the Gentiles, manifested by means of the Church, and which makes them enter and dwell in the house of Shem, according to the promise of God. By the voice of the prophets, the Holy Spirit has announced in advance that all this would be so, in order to strengthen the faith in those who worship God in truth. For that which is absolutely impossible to our nature and for this reason could provoke doubt among men, God had it announced in advance by the prophets. From the moment that the thing is announced long in advance and that it is finally accomplished such as it was predicted, we can conclude that it is God alone who revealed it to us in advance for our salvation. 43. It is necessary and decent to believe God in all things, for in everything God is true, and also that the Son of God exists and that He was not


[p. 692] {#po.12.p692}
վերեւիլ աշխարՏի, ալլ եւ առաջ քան զաշխալճ եղանել ։ Որ առաջինն ճարգարեացաւ Մախես, ասե եբրալեցերեն՝ «Բարեսիք բալա էլովիճ բասան բենուաճ սաճենԹարես» « եւ ալ ըարգճանեալ ի Տալ լեղու՝ « որդի խկդբանն. Տաստատեաց Աստուած ապա վերկին եւ զերկիր » ' « Վալե ալսճ եւ Երեմիալ մարգարե, ասելով ալսպես. « ճառաջ քան կարուսեակ Տնա դքել, եւ առաջ քան զարեգակն անուն քո » ՞. եւ Է ալս քան զաշխարՏիս շինութիւն առաջ քանգի ճիանգաճալե ընդ աշխարճիս եւ աստեղք եղեն. եւ դարձեալ ինքն ասէ. երանելի Է որ Երն առաջ քան ղեղանելն ճարդ նճա ։ Քանզի Աստուծո եղեւ սկիղբն առաջ քան վաշխարծիս շինութիւն Որդին, իսկ Ցեզ ալժճ՝ լորժամ երեւեցաւն. խկ լառաջ քան վալս ճել ոչ եր, որ ոչս գիտեաք ղնա. վասն որոլ եւ աշակերտն նորա Ցովգան պատճելով մեզ` ճը Է Որդին Աստուծոլ, որ Էրե առ. Հաւր առաջ քան ղաշխարծս եղանել, եւ զի ի ճեռն նորա եղեալքս աճենալն եղեն, ասե ալսպես. « Ի սկզբանեն Էր Բանն, եւ Բանն Եր առ. Աստուած, եւ Աստուած եր Բանն. սա եր ի սկլբանեն առ. Աստուած աճենալն ինչ ի Ճեռն նորա եղեւ, եւ առանց նորա եղ՝ եւ եւ ոչինչ » ՝ Ճաւաստադոլնս ցուցեալ, զի-որ ի սկլբանե Բանն Եր ընդ Հաւր, ի ձեռն նորա աճենալնք եղեն՝ սալ Է Որդին նորա
only before His manifestation to the world, but before the creation of the world, as Moses first prophesied, as in the Hebrew he said, "Bareseet bara eloveem basan benovam sament arez". And this is translated in the Armenian language "The Son in the beginning; afterwards God established the heaven and the earth." This thing also the prophet Jeremiah witnesses: "Before the morning star I bore thee, and thy name was before the sun." And this was before the creation of the world, for with the world were also the stars. And again he says, "Blessed is he who existed before becoming a man." For before the creation of the world, the Son was for God in the beginning, but for us when He was manifested. Before this He was not for us, for we did not know Him. Of this His disciple John, telling who the Son of God is, who was with the Father before the world was and by whom were all things created, said: "In the beginning was the Word and the Word was with God and the Word was God, the same was in the beginning with God. All things were made by Him and without Him nothing was made." In a sure manner he showed that the Word who was in the beginning with the Father and by whom were all things, was the Son. 43. It is necessary and decent to believe God in all things, for in everything God is true, and also that the Son of God exists and that He was not
ամենալնի ՞ գաւատալ պարտ եւ արժան Է. քանդի Ճշծարիտ Է ամենայնի Աստուած, եւ զի եղեւ Որդի Աստուծով, հւ սա Է ոչ ճիալն առաջ քան վերեւիլ աշխարՏի, ալլ եւ առաջ քան զաշխալճ եղանել ։ Որ առաջինն ճարգարեացաւ Մախես, ասե եբրալեցերեն՝ «ԲարեսիԹ բալա էլովիճ բասան բենուաճ սաճենԹարես» եւ ալ ըարզճանեալ ի Տալ լեղու՝ « որդի խկդբանն. Տաստատեաց Աստուած ապա վերկին եւ զերկիր » ' « Վալե ալսճ եւ Երեմիալ մարգարե, ասելով ալսպես. « ճառաջ քան զարուսեակ ծնա դքեղ, եւ առաջ քան զարեգակն անուն բո »՞. եւ Է ալս քան վաշխարՏիս շինութիւն առաջ քանգի ճիանգամալե ընդ աշխարճիս եւ աստեղք եղեն. եւ դարձեալ ինքն ասէ. երանելի Է որ Երն առաջ քան ղեղանելն ճարդ նճա ։ Քանզի Աստուծո եղեւ սկիղբն առաջ քան աշխարծիս շինութիւն Որդին, խկ Ցեզ ալժճ՝ լորժամ երեւեցաւն. խկ լառաջ քան զալ ճել ոչ եր, որ ոչս գիտեաք ղնա. վասն որոլ եւ աշակերտն նորա Ցովգան պատճելով ճեզ՝ նը Է Որդին Աստուծոլ, որ Երե առ. Հաւր առաջ քան զաշխարգս եղանել, եւ ղի ի Ճեռն նորա եղեալքս ամենալն եղեն, ասե ալսպես. « Ի սկզբանեն Եր Բանն, եւ Բանն Եր առ. Աստուած, եւ Աստուած եր Բանն. սա եր ի սկլբանեն առ. only before His manifestation to the world, but before the creation of the world, as Moses first prophesied, as in the Hebrew he said, "Bareseet bara eloveem basan benovam sament arez". And this is translated in the Armenian language "The Son in the beginning; afterwards God established the heaven and the earth." This thing also the prophet Jeremiah witnesses: "Before the morning star I bore thee, and thy name was before the sun." And this was before the creation of the world, for with the world were also the stars. And again he says, "Blessed is he who existed before becoming a man." For before the creation of the world, the Son was for God in the beginning, but for us when He was manifested. Before this He was not for us, for we did not know Him. Of this His disciple John, telling who the Son of God is, who was with the Father before the world was and by whom were all things created, said: "In the beginning was the Word and the Word was with God and the Word was God, the same was in the beginning with God. All things were made by Him and without Him nothing was made." In a sure manner he showed that the Word who was in the beginning with the Father and by whom were all things, was the Son.
Հաւր, ի ճեռն նորա աճենալնք եղեն՝ սալ Է Որդին նորա Ճն. եւ դարձեալ Մովսես ճերՃ եկեալ ի ճիաբանութիւն խաւսից զՈրդին ԱստուՏոլ
44. And again Moses speaks of the Son of God approaching to converse


[p. 693] {#po.12.p693}
առ. Աբրագաճ ասէ. « Եւ երեւեցաւ նճա Աստուած առ. կաղնւոջն Մաճբբեին ի ճեջաւ- ըեխ, եւ ի վեր Տալեցեալ աչաւք ետես, եւ ագա երեք արք անցեալ կալին ի վերալ նորա, եւ երկիր եպագ գերկիր եւ ասաց. Տեր, եթե արդարեւ գտի շնորգս առաջի բո» ', եւ վկնխսն զամենայն ինչ խաւսի ընդ Տեաոն, եւ Տեր խաւսի ընղ նմա Սր երկուքն փերիցն Տրեշտակք Էին, իսկ ճին՝ Որդին Աստուծոլ, ընդ որում եւ խաւսեցաւն Աբրա- Տամ, մաղթելով լաղագս Սողոճացւցն՝ ճի կորնչել նոցա, ԹԷ գտցին անդ գոնեա տասն արդարք « Եւ սոքա մինչդեռ խաւսեին, երկու Տրեշտակքն իջանեն ի Սոդոմ, եւ ընդունի զնոսա Ղովթ. եւ ապա ասե դիր. « Եւ Տեր տեղեաց ի վերայ Սոդոճալ եւ Գոճորալ Տըծում եւ Տուր ի Տեառնէ լերկնե »՞. այսինքն Է Որդին, որ եւ խաւսեցաւն ընդ Աբրագամու. իբրու վե Տեր եր, առ լեշխանուցիւն Սողոճացւոցն պատուասի ի Տեառնէ երկնԷ՝ ի Հաւրեն, որ տիրեն աճենեցուն ։ ձե. Եւ դարձեալ Մովսես ճերՃ եկեալ ի ճիաբանութիւն խաւսից լՈրդին ԱստուՏոլ առ. Աբրագաճ ասէ. « Եւ երեւեցաւ նճա Աստուած առ. կաղնւոջն Մաճբբեին ի ճեջաւ- ըեխ, եւ ի վեր Տալեցեալ աչաւք ետես, եւ ագա երեք արք անցեալ կալին ի վերալ նորա, եւ երկիր եպագ գերկիր եւ ասաց. Տեր, եթե արդարեւ գտի շնորգս առաջի բո» ', եւ վկնխսն զամենայն ինչ խաւսի ընդ Տեաոն, եւ Տեր խաւսի ընդ նմա Սր երկուքն փերիցն Տրեշտակք Էին, իսկ ճին՝ Որդին Աստուծոլ, ընդ որում եւ խաւսեցաւն Աբրա- Տամ, մաղթելով լաղագս Սողոճացւցն՝ ճի կորնչել նոցա, ԹԷ գտցին անդ գոնեա տասն արդարք « Եւ սոքա մինչդեռ խաւսեին, երկու Տրեշտակքն իջանեն ի Սոդոմ, եւ ընդունի զնոսա Ղովթ. եւ ապա ասե դիր. « Եւ Տեր տեղեաց ի վերայ Սոդոճալ եւ Գոճորալ Տըծում եւ Տուր ի Տեառնէ լերկնե »՞. այսինքն Է Որդին, որ եւ խաւսեցաւն ընդ Աբրագաճու. իբրու վե Տեր եր, առ լեշխանութիւն Սողոճացւոցն պատուասի ի Տեառնէ երկնԷ՝ ի Հաւրեն, որ տիրեն աճենեցուն ։ Արղ, մարգարե եր Արրագաճ եւ տեսաներ ղֆանդերձեալսն, որ լենելոցն Էն մարդկալին Հեւա դՈրդին Աստուծո, վի խաւսելոց Էր ընդ մարդկան եւ կերակուր ուտելոց ընղ նոսա եւ ապա դատաստանն ի վերալ ածելոց` ի Հաւրեն ալնճանե, որ տիրեն ամենեցուն՝ իշխանութիւն Սողոճացւոցն պատուգասի առեալ ։ Արղ, ճարդարե եր Արրագամ եւ տեսաներ ղֆանդերձեալան, որ լենելոցն Էն մարդկաչին Հեւա դՈրդին Աստուծոչ, զի խաւսելոց Էր ընդ մարդկան եւ կերակուր Հ ուտելոց ընղ նոսա եւ ապա դատաստանն ի վերալ ածելոց` ի Հաւրեն լալնճանե, որ
with Abraham. God appeared to him at noon near the oak of Mamre and he looked up and behold, three men were standing above him, and he bowed to the earth before them and said: "Lord, if indeed I have found grace in thy sight." And in what follows he speaks with the Lord and the Lord with him. Two of these were angels, one was the Son of God with whom Abraham spoke, pleading for the Sodomites, that they might not be destroyed if there should be found for the least there ten righteous men; and as they spoke the two angels descended to Sodom and Lot received them. And then the Scriptures say: "And the Lord rained upon Sodom and Gomorrah brimstone and fire from the Lord out of heaven," that is, the Son who spoke with Abraham. As he was Lord, He received authority to punish the Sodomites from the Lord out of heaven, from the Father who rules over all. But Abraham was a prophet and saw the future which must come to pass, that it was the Son of God in a human form, who must speak with man and eat food with him and bring judgment having received authority from the Father who rules over all to punish the Sodomites. 44. - Again Moses says that the Son of God descended to Abraham and conversed with him: "And God appeared to him under the oak of Mamre in the middle of the day. And lifting his eyes he looked, and behold three men were standing above him. And having prostrated himself on the ground, he worshipped, and said: Lord, if truly I have found grace in thy eyes." And in all that follows he speaks with the Lord, and the Lord converses with him. Now, two of these three personages were angels; but one was the Son of God, with whom Abraham conversed, interceding so that the inhabitants of Sodom would not be destroyed in case one could count there at least ten righteous men. And while they were conversing together, the two angels descended to Sodom, where they were received by Lot. Then, the Scripture adds: "And the Lord rained from heaven upon Sodom and Gomorrah sulfur and fire from the Lord," that is to say the Son, the very one who spoke to Abraham; in his capacity as Lord, it is from the Lord of heaven, from the Father who is the master of all, that he received the power to punish the inhabitants of Sodom. Now, Abraham was a prophet and he saw in the future the things that were to happen, that is to say the Son of God who, under a human form, was to converse with men, eat with them, and become then their judge, he who had received from the Father, from the very one who is the master of all, the power to chastise the inhabitants of Sodom. 44. And again Moses speaks of the Son of God approaching to converse

And in all that follows he speaks with the Lord, and the Lord converses with him. Now, two of these three personages were angels; but one was the Son of God, with whom Abraham conversed, interceding so that the inhabitants of Sodom would not be destroyed in case one could count there at least ten righteous men. And while they were conversing together, the two angels descended to Sodom, where they were received by Lot. Then, the Scripture adds: "And the Lord rained from heaven upon Sodom and Gomorrah sulfur and fire from the Lord," that is to say the Son, the very one who spoke to Abraham; in his capacity as Lord, it is from the Lord of heaven, from the Father who is the master of all, that he received the power to punish the inhabitants of Sodom. Now, Abraham was a prophet and he saw in the future the things that were to happen, that is to say the Son of God who, under a human form, was to converse with men, eat with them, and become then their judge, he who had received from the Father, from the very one who is the master of all, the power to chastise the inhabitants of Sodom. 45. And Jacob going to Mesopotamia saw him who was standing upon the ladder, that is, the tree that was erected from heaven to earth. For upon it believers in Him rise to heaven, for His sufferings are our ascension on


[p. 694] {#po.12.p694}
բարձումն ճեր Է. եւ աճենալն ալսպիսի տեսիլքն զՈրդին Աստուծոլ նշանակեն, խաւ- սելով ընդ ճարդկան եւ լենելով ընդ նոսա ։ Բանգի ճչ եթե Հայն աճենեցուն, որ ոչն տեսանի լաշխարգե, եւ արարողե ամենեցուն, որ ասերն. « Երկին իճ ախոռ Է եւ երկիր պատուանդան ոտից իմոց. ճրպիսի տուն շինեսջիր ինձ եւ կաճ նը տեղի Գանդստեան իճոլ »'. եւ որ ըմբռն ցամաք բռամբ եւ կաւ զերկին՞, սա ի փոբրագոլն վալի անցեալ կացեալ խաւսեր ընդ Աբրագամու. ալ Բանն Աստուծոյ որ ճիշտն Եր ընղ մարդկութեանս, եւ ղճանդերձեալն որ լենելոցն Էին լառաքագոն դեկուցաներ եւ ուսուցաներ զմարդիկ վԱստուՏոլսն ։ Եւ Յակոբ երթալով ի Միջադետս՛ լանըջի տեսանե դնա, զի անցեալ կալը սանդղոցն, ալսինքն Է փայտին, որ Հաստատեալն Եր չերկնե ճինչ չերկիր. քանզի նովաւ ելանեն աւատացեալքն նմա երկինս ։ Բանգի չարչարանքն նորա ի վեր Տամբարձումն
high. All such visions are manifestations of the Son of God, as He speaks with men and lives with them. For the Father of all, who cannot be seen by the world and who is the Creator of all, is not he who says, "Heaven is my throne and the earth is my footstool. What kind of a house will you make for me, or what is the place of my resting?" And He who seizes the dry land in his hand and the heavens in his span was the one who stood in a small space and spoke with Abraham, but it was the Word of God who was always with mankind and revealed beforehand that which would be in the future and taught men divine things. 45. - Jacob also, going to Mesopotamia, saw him in a dream, ascending and descending on a ladder - image of the cross - which went from the earth to heaven. It is by the cross that those who believe in him ascend to heaven. Indeed, the passion of Our Lord is our ascension on high. Now, all these different visions represent the Son of God conversing with men and living in their midst. It is not the Father of all, - the
ճեր Է. եւ աճենալն աապիսի տեսիլքն գՈրդին Աստուծոլ նշանակեն, խաւ- սելով ընդ մարդկան եւ լինելով ընդ նոսա ։ Քանդի ճչ եթէ Հայրն ամենեցուն, որ ոչն տեսանի լաշխարգե, եւ արարողե ամենեցուն, որ ասերն. « Երկին իճ ախոռ. world does not see him, - it is not the Creator of the universe who said: "Heaven is my throne and the earth is the footstool of my feet. What house will you build me and what will be the place of my rest?" nor he "who holds the earth in his hand and measures the heaven with his span"; no, it is not he who came to this corner of the earth to speak with Abraham, it is the Word of God, who did not leave the human race, predicting what was to happen and teaching men the things of God. 46. This is He who spoke in the bush with Moses and said: "Seeing I saw the afflictions of my people which is in Egypt and am come down to deliver them." For the Word of God practiced first upon them that which pertains to us; at that time by an example He showed the future, but now in reality He takes us out of the hard bondage of the heathen and in the wilderness He
ծառալուխենե Հանելով դմեղ եւ դետ ջրոլ լանապատին առատաբար' բղխեցու- ցեալ ի վիճե եւ վեն Է նա, եւ երկոտասան աղբերս տուեալ, ալս Է՝ վարդապետութիւն էրկոլտասան առաքելոցն ։ Եւ գանֆաւան ըերագաւատան վախճանեալ եւ սպառեալ անապատին, խկ զճաւատացնալսն նճա եւ դչարուԹեամբն տղալացեալս ճուծանելով ի ժառանգութիւն գարցն. զոր ոչն Մովսես, ալլ Յիսուս մառանդեալ վիճակեցուցանե, որ եւ փրկեն ղմել Աճաղեկալ ի ձեռն պրկման Ճեռացն իւրոց, եւ ի Հաւրն տարեալ Հան արքալութիւն ։ Սա Է, որ ի ճորենւոցն խաւսեցաւ ընդ Մովսեսի եւ ասաց. « Տեսանելով տեսի դչարչարանս ժողովրդեանն իճոլ՝ որ լԵգիպտոսի, եւ իջի ապրեցուցանել դնոսա»՞ Սա Է, որ ելաներն" եւ իջաներն ի փբկութիւն վշտացելոցն, գանելով ղճեղ` իշխանու- թենեն Նգիպտացւոց, ալաինքն Է լամենալն կռապաշտութենե եւ լամբարշտութենի եւ ի Կարմիր ծովեն փրկելով վճեղ, այսինքն Է ի սպանական Գեքանոսացն խոովու- ենե եւ ի դառն սագեցճանե Հալճոութեանն նոցա փրկելով ղմեղ։ Բանգի ի նոսա առաջագոլն կրքեալ վարժիւր ճերքս Բանին Աստուծոյ, լալնժամ դաղափարաւրեն ցուցելոլ առաջադոլն ղգանդերձեալսն, իսկ ալժմ Ճշճարտապես ի Գեքանոսաց դժնդա- կուխեան ծառալուխենե Հանելով դմեղ եւ դետ ջրոլ լանապատին առատաբար բղխեցու- ցեալ ի վիճե. եւ վեՊե Է նա, եւ երկոտասան աղբերս տուեալ, ալս Է` դվարդապետութիւն էրկոլտասան առաքելոցն ։ Եւ գանֆաւան ըերագաւատան վախճանեալ եւ սպառեալ անապատին, իսկ զքաւատացնալսն նճա եւ լչարութեամբն տղալացեալս ճուծանելով ի ժառանգութիւն գարցն. զոր ոչն Մովսես, ալլ Յիսուս ժառանդեալ վիճակեցուցանե, որ եւ փրկեն ղմեպ Աճաղեկալ ի ձեռն պրկման Ճեռացն իւրոց, եւ ի Հաւրն տարեալ Հանե- արքալունիւն ։
causes the rivers of water to flow abundantly from the rock. But the rock is He. And He gave twelve springs, that is, the teaching of the twelve Apostles. And the unbelievers died and passed away in the wilderness, but those who believed in him and were but children in wickedness, He led into the inheritance of the fathers; it is not Moses but Jesus who leads us to the inheritance, who saves us from Amalek by extending firmly His hands, and leads us and takes us to the Father's kingdom. 46. - It is he again who, in the (burning) bush, spoke to Moses and said to him: "I have seen attentively the tribulations of my people who are in Egypt and I have come down to deliver them." It is he who ascended and descended for the salvation of the afflicted, in order to deliver us from the domination of the Egyptians, that is to say from all idolatry and impiety, in order to save us from the Red Sea, that is to say to preserve us from the bloody discords of the Gentiles and from the bitter scandal of their blasphemies. The Word of God was thus preluding and accustoming himself in some sort to our usages; it is that then he was showing us in advance in figure what was to happen; and here now in reality he has delivered us from the cruel slavery of the Gentiles and has made a river of water gush abundantly in the desert from the rock, and the rock is he. And he made twelve springs flow, namely the doctrine of the twelve apostles. And those who do not want to believe in him will be exhausted and will perish in the desert. As for those who have believed in him and have made themselves children in malice, those he has admitted to the inheritance of the Fathers; but it is not Moses who enters into participation of this inheritance and who makes the sharing of it, it is Jesus who delivers us from Amalek by the extension of his arms (on the cross), leads us and raises us to the kingdom of his Father. 46. This is He who spoke in the bush with Moses and said: "Seeing I saw the afflictions of my people which is in Egypt and am come down to deliver them." This is He who ascends and descends for the salvation of the troubled, taking us out of the dominion of the Egyptians, that is, from all idolatry and ungodliness, and delivering us from the Red Sea, that is, from the fatal disorder of the heathen and from the bitter stumblings of their blasphemies. For the Word of God practiced first upon them that which pertains to us; at that time by an example He showed the future, but now in reality He takes us out of the hard bondage of the heathen and in the wilderness He
Արդ` Տեր Է Հարն, եւ Տեր Է Որդին, եւ Աստուած Հայրն եւ Աստուած Որդին. քանդի Աստուծո Ֆնեալն Աստուած Է Եւ աապես, ըստ գոլացութեանն եւ զաւրութեան իւրոլ Էութեանն՝ ճի Աստուած ցու՞ ցանի, եւ Է խկ ըստ տնաւրեն ճատակարարուխեան փրկուցեանս ճերոլ եւ Որդի եւ Հայը. վասն դի անտեսանելի Է եւ անճատոլց եղելոց Հալրն աճենեցուն, ի Ճեռն Որդւոն պարտ Է ունել դառ ի Հայրն նուաճաւմն առաջածութեանն գանդերձեալքն ճերձենալ առ Աստուած ։ Եւ եւս լաղտնի երեւելադոլն պալծառ. Դաւիթ լաղադս Հաւրն եւ Որդւոլ աապես ասե. « Աթոո քո Աստուած լաւիտեանս լաւիտենից. սիրեցեր զարդարութիւն, ատեցեր անիրաւութիւն. վասն աաորիկ աւծ զել Աստուած եւղ ուրախուխեան առաւել քան վկցորդս քո» ' » Քանդի զՈրդի, իբրու գի Աստուած Է, առնուլ ի Հաւրեն, այսինքն
causes the rivers of water to flow abundantly from the rock. But the rock is He. And He gave twelve springs, that is, the teaching of the twelve Apostles. And the unbelievers died and passed away in the wilderness, but those who believed in him and were but children in wickedness, He led into the inheritance of the fathers; it is not Moses but Jesus who leads us to the inheritance, who saves us from Amalek by extending firmly His hands, and leads us and takes us to the Father's kingdom. 46. - It is he again who, in the (burning) bush, spoke to Moses and said to him: "I have seen attentively the tribulations of my people who are in Egypt and I have come down to deliver them." It is he who ascended and descended for the salvation of the afflicted, in order to deliver us from the domination of the Egyptians, that is to say from all idolatry and impiety, in order to save us from the Red Sea, that is to say to preserve us from the bloody discords of the Gentiles and from the bitter scandal of their blasphemies. The Word of God was thus preluding and accustoming himself in some sort to our usages; it is that then he was showing us in advance in figure what was to happen; and here now in reality he has delivered us from the cruel slavery of the Gentiles and has made a river of water gush abundantly in the desert from the rock, and the rock is he. And he made twelve springs flow, namely the doctrine of the twelve apostles. And those who do not want to believe in him will be exhausted and will perish in the desert. As for those who have believed in him and have made themselves children in malice, those he has admitted to the inheritance of the Fathers; but it is not Moses who enters into participation of this inheritance and who makes the sharing of it, it is Jesus who delivers us from Amalek by the extension of his arms (on the cross), leads us and raises us to the kingdom of his Father. 47. Now the Father is Lord and the Son is Lord, and the Father is God and the Son is God, for that which is born of God is God, and so, according to the essence and power of His being, there is shown one God who is yet both Son and Father according to the economy of our salvation. Since the Father of all is invisible and intangible to beings, it is necessary that those who are ordained to draw nigh to God, receive grace to be brought nigh to the Father by means of the Son. In a plain and clear way David speaks thus concerning the Father and the Son: "Thy throne, Oh God, is forever and ever, thou lovest righteousness and hatest iniquity. Therefore thy God hath anointed thee with the oil of gladness above thy fellows." For the Son, since He is God, receives from the Father who is God, the throne of eternity and the oil of anointing more than His fellows. And the oil of


[p. 695] {#po.12.p695}
Աստուծո զախոռն լլաւիտենական արքայութեանն եւ վեւղն վաւծճանն դառաւելն քան դկցորդսն նորա ։ Եւ եւղ աւծճան Է Հոդին, որով աւծեալն Ե, եւ կցորդքն նորա ճարդարեք եւ արդարք եւ առաքեալը եւ ամենեքին, որք առնուն կցորդութիւն վզարքալուխեանն նորա, ալաինքն Է աշակերտքն նորա ։ Արդ` Տեր Է Հարն, եւ Տեր Է Որդին, եւ Աստուած Հայրն եւ Աստուած Որդին. քանդի Աստուծո Ֆնեալն Աստուած Է Եւ աապես, ըստ գոլացութեանն եւ զաւրութեան իւրոլ Էութեանն՝ ճի Աստուած ցու՞ ցանի, եւ Է խկ ըստ տնաւրեն ճատակարարուխեան փրկուցեանս ճերոլ եւ Որդի եւ Հայը. վասն դի անտեսանելի Է եւ անճատոլց եղելոց Հալրն աճենեցուն, ի Ճեռն Որդւոն պարտ Է ունել դառ ի Հայրն նուաճաւմն առաջածութեանն գանդերձեալքն ճերձենալ առ Աստուած ։ Եւ եւս լաղտնի երեւելադոլն պալծառ. Դաւիթ լաղադս Հաւրն եւ Որդւոլ աապես ասե. « Աթոո քո Աստուած լաւիտեանս լաւիտենից. սիրեցեր զարդարութիւն, ատեցեր
anointing is the Spirit with which He is anointed, and His fellows are the prophets and righteous men and apostles and all who receive the communication of His kingdom, that is, who are his disciples. 47. - Now, the Father is Lord, and the Son is Lord. The Father is God, and the Son is God; for he who is born of God is God. Thus, by the very essence and nature of his being, it is demonstrated that there is only one God, although according to the economy of our redemption, there is both a Son and a Father. Since the Father of all is invisible and inaccessible to created beings, it is necessary, for those who must approach God, to receive, by means of the Son, the grace of being presented to the Father. And more manifestly still and more clearly, David says concerning the Father and the Son: "Thy throne, O God, is established for ages of ages. Thou hast loved justice and hated iniquity. Therefore God hath anointed thee with an oil of gladness, in preference to thy companions." Since he is God, indeed, the Son must receive from the Father, that is to say from God, the eternal throne of heaven and be consecrated with the oil of anointing,


[p. 696] {#po.12.p696}
անիրաւութիւն. վասն աաորիկ աւծ զել Աստուած եւղ ուրախուխեան առաւել քան վկցորդս քո» ' » Քանդի զՈրդի, իբրու գի Աստուած Է, առնուլ ի Հաւրեն, այսինքն Աստուծո զախոռն լլաւիտենական արքայութեանն եւ վեւղն վաւծճանն դառաւելն քան դկցորդսն նորա ։ Եւ եւղ աւծճան Է Հոդին, որով աւծեալն Ե, եւ կցորդքն նորա ճարդարեք եւ արդարք եւ առաքեալը եւ ամենեքին, որք առնուն կցորդութիւն վզարքալուխեանն նորա, ալաինքն Է աշակերտքն նորա ։
much more than his companions. The oil of anointing is the Spirit poured out upon him; and his companions are the prophets, the righteous, the apostles, and all those who receive participation in his kingdom, that is to say his disciples. 47. Now the Father is Lord and the Son is Lord, and the Father is God and the Son is God, for that which is born of God is God, and so, according to the essence and power of His being, there is shown one God who is yet both Son and Father according to the economy of our salvation. Since the Father of all is invisible and intangible to beings, it is necessary that those who are ordained to draw nigh to God, receive grace to be brought nigh to the Father by means of the Son. In a plain and clear way David speaks thus concerning the Father and the Son: "Thy throne, Oh God, is forever and ever, thou lovest righteousness and hatest iniquity. Therefore thy God hath anointed thee with the oil of gladness above thy fellows." For the Son, since He is God, receives from the Father who is God, the throne of eternity and the oil of anointing more than His fellows. And the oil of
anointing is the Spirit with which He is anointed, and His fellows are the prophets and righteous men and apostles and all who receive the communication of His kingdom, that is, who are his disciples. 47. - Now, the Father is Lord, and the Son is Lord. The Father is God, and the Son is God; for he who is born of God is God. Thus, by the very essence and nature of his being, it is demonstrated that there is only one God, although according to the economy of our redemption, there is both a Son and a Father. Since the Father of all is invisible and inaccessible to created beings, it is necessary, for those who must approach God, to receive, by means of the Son, the grace of being presented to the Father. And more manifestly still and more clearly, David says concerning the Father and the Son: "Thy throne, O God, is established for ages of ages. Thou hast loved justice and hated iniquity. Therefore God hath anointed thee with an oil of gladness, in preference to thy companions." Since he is God, indeed, the Son must receive from the Father, that is to say from God, the eternal throne of heaven and be consecrated with the oil of anointing,

աւուրն զաւրու- Ր բ գոց « ենդ բել ի սկվբան աւուրն դուր ըեան բո, ի լուսաւորուցեան սրբոցն՝ լարդանդե առաջ քան՝ լար)ուսեակ Տնալ զթեղ Երդուաւ Տեր եւ ոչ լղջասցի. դու ես քագանայ չաւիտեան ըստ ղասուն Մելքի- սեղդեկի, եւ Տեր լաջճե բուճե ։ Ջաղխեաց յաւուր բարկուխեան ըագաւորս, դատեսցի ի Տեթանոսս, լցցե կործանեալս եւ ջաղխեսցե դգլուխս բապմաց լերկիր ։ Յուղխե ի ճանապարՏի ալբցե, վասն այորիկ բարձրացուսցե զգլուխ »' « Ար ի ձեռն ալսր նախառաջին զնա եղանել դեկոչց եւ տիրելն դնա Հեքանոսաց եւ դատելն զաճենալն Ցարդիկ եւ ղնագաւորնն, ղալժճու ատեցողսն գնա եւ զանուն նորա Հալածողս, քանզի սոքա ըշնաճիք նորա. եւ քաՏանալ ասացեալ դնա չաւիտենական Աստուծով, ղանճա- Տութիւն նորա ապդեաց ։ Եւ վասն աաորիկ ասաց. « Յուղխե արբցե ի ճանապարգի, վասն աաորիկ բարձրացուսցե զգլուխ ». դրստ մարդկուխեանն նորա դեկուցանե եւ վըստ նուատտուխեանն եւ անփառութեանն բարձրութիւն փառաւր ։ Եւ դարձեալ ասէ Դաւիթ. « ԱսԷ Տեր ցՏԵր իճ, նիստ ընդ աջճե իճմե, ճինչ եդից դնշնաճիս քո պատուանդան ոտից քոց « Փաւաղան դաւրութեան առաքեսցե ՏԷր ի Սիովխե, եւ տիրեա ի ճեջ ըշնաճիք նորա. եւ քաճանալ ասացեալ դնա չաւիտենական Աստուծով, ղանճա- Հութիւն նորա ապդեաց ։ Եւ վասն աաորիկ ասաց. « Յուղխե արբցե ի ճանապարգի, վասն աաորիկ բարձրացուսցե զգլուխ ». դրստ մարդկուխեանն նորա պեկուցանե եւ վըստ նուատտուխեանն եւ անփառութեանն բարձրութիւն փառաւր ։
much more than his companions. The oil of anointing is the Spirit poured out upon him; and his companions are the prophets, the righteous, the apostles, and all those who receive participation in his kingdom, that is to say his disciples. 48. And again David says: "The Lord said unto my Lord, Sit at my right hand until I make thy enemies thy footstool. The Lord shall send the rod of strength out of Zion: rule thou in the midst of thy enemies. Thy power was with thee in the beginning of days in the illumination of the holy ones; I bore thee from the womb before the morning star. The Lord hath sworn and will not repent; thou art a priest for ever after the order of Melchizedek. The Lord is at thy right hand. He struck the kings in the day of His wrath. He shall judge among the heathen: they shall fill up with the fallen ones: and the heads of many He shall smite upon the earth. He shall drink of the brook by the way therefore shall He lift up the head." By this He showed that He has been before all, as well as His conquering the heathen and judging all men and even kings, who now hate Him and persecute His name, for they are His enemies. And calling Him the eternal priest of God signifies His immortality. For this He said, "He shall drink of the brook by the way, therefore shall He lift up the head," signifies His humanity and the glorious exaltation after His humiliation and inglorious state. 48. - And David says again: "The Lord said to my Lord: Sit at my right hand, until I make thy enemies the footstool of thy feet. The Lord shall send forth from Zion the scepter of strength; and be the master in the midst of thy enemies. I am with thee in the beginning in the day of thy strength in the splendor of the saints; before the morning star, I have begotten thee from my womb. The Lord has sworn it and he will not repent of it: Thou art a priest forever according to the order of Melchizedek, and the Lord is at thy right hand. He has broken the kings in the day of his wrath; he shall exercise his judgment among the nations: he shall heap up the corpses and he shall crush the head of many on the earth. He shall drink from the torrent on the way; therefore he shall lift up the head." And by these words he has proved that Christ is the first before all things, that he is the master of the Gentiles and that he exercises his judgment over all men and over the kings, who hate him now and persecute his name, for such are his enemies. And by naming him eternal priest of God, he declares that he is immortal. And when he says: "He drank from the torrent in the way; therefore he shall lift up the head," he speaks of his humanity which, by reason of his abasement and his humiliations, shall enjoy a glorious elevation. 48. And again David says: "The Lord said unto my Lord, Sit at my right hand until I make thy enemies thy footstool. The Lord shall send the rod of strength out of Zion: rule thou in the midst of thy enemies. Thy power was with thee in the beginning of days in the illumination of the holy ones; I bore thee from the womb before the morning star. The Lord hath sworn and will not repent; thou art a priest for ever after the order of Melchizedek. The Lord is at thy right hand. He struck the kings in the day of His wrath. He shall judge among the heathen: they shall fill up with the fallen ones: and the heads of many He shall smite upon the earth. He shall drink of the brook by the way therefore shall He lift up the head." By this He showed that He has been before all, as well as His conquering the heathen and judging all men and even kings, who now hate Him and persecute His name, for they are His enemies. And calling Him the eternal priest of God signifies His immortality. For this He said, "He shall drink of the brook by the way, therefore shall He lift up the head," signifies His humanity and the glorious exaltation after His humiliation and inglorious state. 48. - And David says again: "The Lord said to my Lord: Sit at my right hand, until I make thy enemies the footstool of thy feet. The Lord shall send forth from Zion the scepter of strength; and be the master in the midst of thy enemies. I am with thee in the beginning in the day of thy strength in the splendor of the saints; before the morning star, I have begotten thee from my womb. The Lord has sworn it and he will not repent of it: Thou art a priest forever according to the order of Melchizedek, and the Lord is at thy right hand. He has broken the kings in the day of his wrath; he shall exercise his judgment among the nations; he shall heap up the corpses and he shall crush the head of many on the earth. He shall drink from the torrent on the way; therefore he shall lift up the head." And by these words he has proved that Christ is the first before all things, that he is the master of the Gentiles and that he exercises his judgment over all men and over the kings, who hate him now and persecute his name, for such are his enemies. And by naming him eternal priest of God, he declares that he is immortal. And when he says: "He drank from the torrent in the way; therefore he shall lift up the head," he speaks of his humanity which, by reason of his abasement and his humiliations, shall enjoy a glorious elevation.
Եւ դարձեալ Եսայի մարգարե ասե. « Ալսպես ասէ ՏԷր Աստուած ցաւծեալ իճ ցՏել, զորոլ կալալ վաջոլե, լսել առաջի նորա դճեխանոաս». եւ եթե դիճրդ եւ լիստոսն ասի Որդին Աստուծոյ եւ թագաւոր Տեխանոսաց, որ Է աճենալն մարդկան, եւ պի Որդի Աստուճոլ եւ ասի եւ Է եւ խագաւոր աճենեցուն, ասպես Դաւիթ ասե «Տեր ասաց առ. իս, որդի իճ ես. ես ճերկ Տնալ լքեզ, խնդրեմ լինեն եւ տալց քե վճե՝քանոսս զժառանգուխիւն քո եւ լկալուածսն դտիեզերս երկրի »՞ Է Եւ դարձեալ Եսայի մարգարե ասե. « Ալսպես ասէ ՏԷր Աստուած ցաւծեալ իճ ցՏել, զորոլ կալալ վաջոլն, լսել առաջի նորա ղճեխանոսա»'. եւ եթե դինրդ եւ լիստոսն ասի Որդին Աստուծոյ եւ թագաւոր Տեխանոսաց, որ Է աճենալն մարդկան, եւ պի Որդի Աստուճոլ եւ ասի եւ Է եւ խագաւոր աճենեցուն, ասպես Դաւիթ ասէ. «Տեր ասաց առ. իս, որդի իճ ես. ես մերկ ծնայ լքել, խնդրեմ: լինեն եւ տալց քեզ վճե՝քանոսս զժառանգուխիւն քո եւ լկալուածան վտիեղերս երկրի »՞ ։ Ալոքիկ ճչ ի Դաւիք ասացան, քանզի նչ ի Տեքանոսաց, եւ նչ տիեզերաց իշխեաց, ավ միալն Հրեից » Արդ` ալտ Է, աւծելոլն խոստումնն ըագաւորել ի տիեզերց երկրի Որդւոլն Աստուճոլ Է, զոր ինքն Դաւիթ իւը Տեր խոստովանե` ալսպես ասելով. « ԱսԷ
49. And again the prophet Isaiah says: "Thus says the Lord God to my anointed Lord whose right hand I have holden, to bring into obedience to him the nations," and further on, that Christ is called the Son of God, and the king of nations, that is, of all men. But also David says, that he is called and is Son of God and king of nations: so David says, "The Lord said to me, Thou art my son, I today begot thee: Ask of me and I will give thee the nations for thy inheritance and for thy possession the ends of the earth." These things are not said of David, for he did not rule over the nations nor the whole earth but only over the Jews. Therefore it is evident that the promise to the anointed one to rule over the ends of the earth has relation to the Son of God whom David himself confesses as his Lord saying: "The Lord said to my Lord: Sit on my right hand," and so on, as we said before. For he refers to the Father's speaking of the Son, as a little before this we showed in the case of Isaiah that he also says: "God says to my Anointed Lord, to bring into obedience to Him the nations." Because the same promise is given by two prophets that He would be king, therefore the word of God is directed to the same one, that is, I say, to Christ the Son of God. For David says: "the Lord says to Me." It is necessary to say that it is not David nor one of the other prophets who speaks of himself, for it is not man

Տեր ցեր իճ, նիստ ընդ աջճե իճճե»՞, եւվկնիսն, որպես լառաջագոլնն ասացաք անդի գալը ընդ Որղւոլ խաւսել ասե, որպես եւ սակաւ մի լառաջադոլն դետալի յուցաք, գի ասեր ալսպես. «Ասէ Աստուած ցաւճեալ իճ ցՏեր, լսել առաջի նորա վճեքանոսս »* ։ Քանզի նոն խոստումն ի Ճեռն երկուցունց մարգարեիցն, թագաւո գոլ դնա. ապա ուրեմն առ ճի եւ առ նոն խաւսքն Աստուծոյ են, ալինքն ասեմ առ. Քրիստոս Որդին Աստուծոյ ։ Վասն վի Դաւիթ ասե. « Տեր ասաց առ խա». Տարկաւոը Է ասել, պի ոչ Դաւիթ Է որ խաւսին եւ մչ ալ ոք ի ճարգարեիցն ինքն
who speaks prophecies but the Spirit of God, who, giving Himself a fashion and appearance according to the future persons, speaks in the prophets, and sometimes He presents the word as from Christ and at other times as from the Father. 49. - In his turn, the prophet Isaiah said: "Thus speaks God the Lord to my Anointed the Lord, whom I have taken by the right hand to reduce to obedience before him the nations." փեքենէ.. քանգի ոչ մարդ Ե, որ ասե լժարզարեունիւան, ալ Աստուծո Հոգին՝ կեր- պարանեալ եւ Ճեւայուցեալ զինքն նան առաջիկայ դիմացն, ի ճարգարեսն խաւսեր, երբեմն ի Քրիստոսէ եւ երբեմն ի Հաւրէ առներ վբանսն Ալտքիկ ճչ ի Դաւիք ասացան, քանզի ճչ ի Տեքանոսաց, եւ ճչ տիեզերաց իշխեաց, ավ միալն Հրեից » Արը՝ լալտ Է, աւծելոլն խոստումնն ըագաւորել ի տիեզերց երկրի Որդւոլն Աստուճոլ Է, զոր ինքն Դաւիթ իւը Տեր խոստովանե` ալսպես ասելով. « ԱսԷ Տեր ցեր իճ, նիստ ընդ աջճե իճճե»՝, եւվկնիսն, որպես լառաջագոլնն ասացաք անդի գալը ընդ Որղւոլ խաւսել ասե, որպես եւ սակաւ մի լառաջադոլն դետալի յուցաք, վե ասեր ալսպես. «Աս Աստուած ցաւճեալ իճ ցՏեր, լսել առաջի նորա վճեքանոսս» ։ Քանզի նոն խոստումն ի Ճեռն երկուցունց մարգարեիցն, քագաւո գոլ դնա. ապա ուրեմն առ ճի եւ առ նոն խաւսքն Աստուծոյ են, ալինքն ասեմ առ Քրիստոս Որդին Աստուծոլ ։ Վասն վի Դաւիթ ասե. «Տեր ասաց առ իս». Գարկաւոը Է ասել զե ոչ Դաւիթ Է որ խաւսին եւ ճչ ալլ ոբ ի մարգարեիցն ինքն
"The Lord said to me: Thou art my Son, I have begotten thee today. Ask of me and I will give thee the nations as an inheritance and for domain the ends of the earth." These words do not address David who governed neither the Gentiles, nor the universe, and only reigned over the Jews. It is evident that the promise made to Christ to reign over the whole universe relates to the Son of God. David himself recognizes him as his Lord, when he expresses himself thus: "The Lord said to my Lord: Sit at my right hand" and the rest that we have cited above. For he says that the Father speaks with the Son. This is what, moreover, we have seen previously in Isaiah, whose words are: "God says to my Anointed the Lord ... to reduce to obedience before him the nations." Indeed, it is the same promise of royalty which is made by the two prophets; consequently, the words of God address one and the same person, I mean Christ, Son of God. David expresses himself thus: "The Lord said to me." It must be agreed that it is neither David, nor any of the other prophets who speaks of himself; for it is not man who prophesies,

49. And again the prophet Isaiah says: "Thus says the Lord God to my anointed Lord whose right hand I have holden, to bring into obedience to him the nations," and further on, that Christ is called the Son of God, and the king of nations, that is, of all men. But also David says, that he is called and is Son of God and king of nations: so David says, "The Lord said to me, Thou art my son, I today begot thee: Ask of me and I will give thee the nations for thy inheritance and for thy possession the ends of the earth." These things are not said of David, for he did not rule over the nations nor the whole earth but only over the Jews. Therefore it is evident that the promise to the anointed one to rule over the ends of the earth has relation to the Son of God whom David himself confesses as his Lord saying: "The Lord said to my Lord: Sit on my right hand," and so on, as we said before. For he refers to the Father's speaking of the Son, as a little before this we showed in the case of Isaiah that he also says: "God says to my Anointed Lord, to bring into obedience to Him the nations." Because the same promise is given by two prophets that He would be king, therefore the word of God is directed to the same one, that is, I say, to Christ the Son of God. For David says: "the Lord says to Me." It is necessary to say that it is not David nor one of the other prophets who speaks of himself, for it is not man
who speaks prophecies but the Spirit of God, who, giving Himself a fashion and appearance according to the future persons, speaks in the prophets, and sometimes He presents the word as from Christ and at other times as from the Father. 49. - In his turn, the prophet Isaiah said: "Thus speaks God the Lord to my Anointed the Lord, whom I have taken by the right hand to reduce to obedience before him the nations." How is Christ called the Son of God and the king of the Gentiles, that is to say of all men? David says that (Christ) is called and is (really) Son of God and king of all; here are his words:
Արդ՝ ի ճաճ դիպաւորապես րիստոս ի Ճեռն Դաւմալ ասէ, ինքն ընդ ինքեան վճաւրն խաւսել, եւ ի ճագ արժանաւորապես եւ զայլսն եւս ինքն լաղագս իւր ի ձեռն մարգա ՞ ըեիցն ասե, որպես եւ ալան եւ Եսալետւ լաւրինակս գալս. « Եւալժճ ալսպես ասե Տեր, որ Ետտեղծն զիս ծառայ իւր որովալնե, ժողովել Յակովբ: եւ լիսրալել առ. նա ժողովել, եւ. փառաւորեցայց առաջի Տեառն եւ Աստուած իճ եղեցի ինճ զաւրու- իւն» Եւ ասաց` ՅԵՏ եղեցի քեզ կոչել Տառալ իճ, կացուցանել եւ. Հաստատել զազգս Յակովբայ եւ ղցրումն խսրալելի ղարճուցանել. եւ եղի դքեզ ի լս Տեխանոսաց, գոլ բեզ է ֆբկունիւն, ճինչ ի վերջեն երկիր »' Արդ՝ ի ճաճ դիպաւորապես րիստոս ի Ճեռն Դաւխալ ասէ, ինքն ընդ ինքեան վճարն խաւսել, եւ ի ճագ արժանաւորապես եւ զայլսն եւս ինքն լաղագս իւր ի Հեռն մարգա ՞ ըեիցն ասե, որպես եւ լալլսն եւ Նսալետւ ղաւրինակս գալս. « Եւալժճ ալսպես ասե Տեր, որ Ետտեղծն զիս ծառալ իւր որովալնե, ժողովել Յակովբ եւ լիսրալել առ. նա ժողովել, եւ. փառաւորեցայց առաջի Տեառն եւ Աստուած իճ եղեցի ինճ զաւրու- իւն ։ Եւ ասաց` ՅԵՏ եղեցի քեզ կոչլ Տառալ իճ, կացուցանել եւ. Հաստատել զազգս Յակովբայ եւ զցրումն խսրալելի դարձուցանել. եւ եղի դքեզ ի լս Տեխանոսաց, գոլ բեզ է ֆբկունիւն, ճինչ ի վերջեն երկիր »' »
"The Lord said to me: Thou art my Son, I have begotten thee today. Ask of me and I will give thee the nations as an inheritance and for domain the ends of the earth." These words do not address David who governed neither the Gentiles, nor the universe, and only reigned over the Jews. It is evident that the promise made to Christ to reign over the whole universe relates to the Son of God. David himself recognizes him as his Lord, when he expresses himself thus: "The Lord said to my Lord: Sit at my right hand" and the rest that we have cited above. For he says that the Father speaks with the Son. This is what, moreover, we have seen previously in Isaiah, whose words are: "God says to my Anointed the Lord ... to reduce to obedience before him the nations." Indeed, it is the same promise of royalty which is made by the two prophets; consequently, the words of God address one and the same person, I mean Christ, Son of God. David expresses himself thus: "The Lord said to me." It must be agreed that it is neither David, nor any of the other prophets who speaks of himself; for it is not man who prophesies, it is the Spirit of God who, through the intermediary of the prophets, assumes the figure and the form of the personage in question, and speaks sometimes in the name of Christ and sometimes in the name of the Father. 50. But in a very fitting manner, Christ says by David that the Father speaks with Him. And He properly says other things of Himself by the prophets. Among other things by Isaiah he speaks thus: "And now thus says the Lord, who created me his servant from the womb to gather Jacob and to gather Israel unto Him, and I will be glorified before the Lord and my God shall be my strength. And He said: It will be a great thing for thee to be called my servant, to raise up and establish the race of Jacob and to return the dispersion of Israel. I have given thee for a light to the Gentiles that thou mayst be salvation unto the end of the earth." 50. - Now Christ says with much appropriateness, by the mouth of David, that his Father spoke to him and very honorably; and (Christ) also says by the prophets the other things which relate to him; and to cite only one, here is what one reads in Isaiah: "And now the Lord speaks thus, he who created me from the womb (of my mother) to be his servant, in order to bring back to him Jacob, and to bring back to him Israel. And I have been honored before the Lord, and my God shall be my strength. And he said: It will be great for thee to be called my servant, to raise up and re-establish the tribes of Jacob and bring back dispersed Israel. And I have established thee to be the light of the nations, and so that thou mayst be for salvation unto the ends of the earth."
5Լ. մանի աստանաւը նախառաջին, ի առաջ եր Աստուծոլ Որդին` լալնճանե Է, պի Հայրն խաւսեր ընդ նմա. եւ առաջ քան դծնանիլն դնա երեւելի ծարդկան արար, եւ ապա՝ գի Տնանելի զնա ի ճարդկան ճարդ պարտ եւ արժան Է լենել ։ Եւ վի նոն ինքն Աստուած լորովալնե ստեղծանե- լեա, ալսինքն Ե, զի ի Հողւոլ Աստու- Տոլ Տեցի, եւ վի աճենալն մարդկան Տեր Է եւ փրկիչ գաւատացելոցն նմա Հրեից եւ
that his Father spoke to him and very honorably; and (Christ) also says by the prophets the other things which relate to him; and to cite only one, here is what one reads in Isaiah: "And now the Lord speaks thus, he who created me from the womb (of my mother) to be his servant, in order to bring back to him Jacob, and to bring back to him Israel. And I have been honored before the Lord, and my God shall be my strength. And he said: It will be great for thee to be called my servant, to raise up and re-establish the tribes of Jacob and bring back dispersed Israel. And I have established thee to be the light of the nations, and so that thou mayst be for salvation unto the ends of the earth." 51. For here first of all, it is evident that the Son of God was preexistent in that the Father speaks with Him and in that before His birth He appeared to men. Also that afterwards He must be born a man among men. And that God Himself creates Him from the womb means that He must be born of the Spirit of God and that he is Lord of all men and Saviour of believers whether Jews or others. For the people of the Jews is called Israel in the

ալոցն » Բանգի Իսրայել Տրեիցն ժողովուրդ կոչի եբրայական խաւսիւքն ի Ցակովրալ Տաւրե, որ եւ առաջինն Իսրայել կոչեցաւ. եւ Հեթանոս կոչե զաճենալն լճարդիկ եւ առայ զինքն Հաւրն Որդին ասել, վասն առ Հայրն Հնազանդութեան, վի աճենալն որդի Տառալ Ե Հաւրն իւրոլ եւ առ. ճարդկան » ԾԼ. մանի աստանաւը նախառաջին, դի առաջ եր Աստուծոլ Որդին` լալնմանե Է, զի Հան խաւսեր ընդ նմա. եւ առաջ քան դծնանիլն զեա երեւելի ճարդկան արար, եւ ապա՝ դի ծնանելի զնա ի մարդկան մարդ պարտ եւ արժան Է լենել » Եւ վի նոլն ինքն Աստուած լորովալնե ստեղծանե դնա, ալաինքն Է, պի ի Հոգւոլ Աստու Տոլ ծնցի, եւ վի ամենալն ճարդկան Տեր Ե եւ փրկիչ գաւատացելոցն նճա Հրեից եւ ալոցն » Բանգի Իսրայել Տրեիցն ժողովուրդ կոչի եբրայական խաւսիւքն ի Ցակովրալ Տաւրե, որ եւ առաջինն Իսրայել կոչեցաւ. եւ Հեթանոս կոչե զաճենալն լճարդիկ
Hebrew language for their father Jacob was called Israel first, and "nations" is a name for all men. And the Son calls himself the "servant" of the Father, because of His obedience to the Father, for among men also every son is the servant of his father? 51. - Now here first of all is marked the preexistence of the Son of God. It results from this fact that the Father converses with him and from the fact that before his birth, he manifests him to men. Then (one must conclude) that (the Son of God) will certainly be man in so far as he must draw his origin from men, and that God himself must create him from the womb (of a Virgin), that is to say that he will be born (by the operation) of the Spirit of God. It follows finally that he is the Lord of all men and the savior of those who believe in him, Jews and others. Indeed, the Jewish people is in the Hebrew language called Israel from its father Jacob, who the first was called Israel; and all (other) men are named Gentiles. And the Son takes the name of servant of the Father, because of his
